    10	use std::collections::BTreeMap;
    11	fn refuse(s: impl Into<String>) -> RuntimeError {
    12	    RuntimeError::Structural {
    13	        code: "E_ADOPT",
    14	        message: s.into(),
    15	    }
    16	}
    17	fn asset(k: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    18	    match k {
    19	        ColumnType::Optional(k)
    20	        | ColumnType::List { of: k, .. }
    21	        | ColumnType::ListRange { of: k, .. } => asset(k),
    22	        ColumnType::Image { .. } | ColumnType::File { .. } | ColumnType::Video { .. } => true,
    23	        _ => false,
    24	    }
    25	}
    26	fn fields(row: &mut Value) -> Result<&mut crate::value::ObjectFields, RuntimeError> {
    27	    match row {
    28	        Value::Row(f) | Value::Map(f) => Ok(f),
    29	        _ => Err(refuse("invalid extraction row")),
    30	    }
    31	}
    32	fn key(tx:&Tx<'_>,e:&Extraction)->Result<String,RuntimeError>{
    33	    let parent=tx.num(&e.parent)?;let child=tx.num(&e.child)?;
    34	    let aliases:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_asset_column_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    35	    let origin=|t:i64,c:&str|->Result<String,RuntimeError>{
    36	        if !aliases{return Ok(c.into())}
    37	        Ok(tx.conn().query_row("SELECT origin FROM _asset_column_renames WHERE t=?1 AND name=?2",params![t,c],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?.unwrap_or_else(||c.into()))
    38	    };
    39	    let fields=e.fields.iter().map(|(dest,source)|Ok((origin(child,dest)?,origin(parent,source)?))).collect::<Result<BTreeMap<_,_>,RuntimeError>>()?;
    40	    let base=(parent,child,origin(child,&e.reference)?,fields);
    41	    Ok(if e.each {crate::value::digest(&("each-v1",base))}else{crate::value::digest(&base)})
    42	}
    43	fn exists(tx: &Tx<'_>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    44	    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(
    45	        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_extraction_rows')",
    46	        [],
    47	        |r| r.get(0),
    48	    )?)
    49	}
    50	fn load(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
    51	    let bytes: Option<i64> = tx
    52	        .conn()
    53	        .query_row(
    54	            "SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
    55	            params![t, id],
    56	            |r| r.get(0),
    57	        )
    58	        .optional()?;
    59	    let Some(bytes) = bytes else { return Ok(None) };
    60	    Meter::new(Ceilings::job(), Cut::Abort).heap(
    61	        (bytes.max(0) as u64)
    62	            .saturating_mul(64)
    63	            .saturating_add(8192),
    64	    )?;
    65	    let text: String = tx.conn().query_row(
    66	        "SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
    67	        params![t, id],
    68	        |r| r.get(0),
    69	    )?;
    70	    serde_json::from_str(&text)
    71	        .map(Some)
    72	        .map_err(|e| refuse(e.to_string()))
    73	}
    74	fn write(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    75	    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(t,id) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data",params![t,id,canonical_json(row)])?;
    76	    Ok(())
    77	}
    78	fn value(
    79	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    80	    t: i64,
    81	    id: &str,
    82	    row: &Value,
    83	    c: &str,
    84	) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
    85	    if let Ok(v) = row.field(c) {
    86	        return Ok(Some(v.clone()));
    87	    }
    88	    let text: Option<String> = tx
    89	        .conn()
    90	        .query_row(
    91	            "SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
    92	            params![t, id, c],
    93	            |r| r.get(0),
    94	        )
    95	        .optional()?;
    96	    text.map(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| refuse(e.to_string())))
    97	        .transpose()
    98	}
    99	fn transfer(
   100	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   101	    v: &Value,
   102	    from: (&str, &str, &str),
   103	    to: (&str, &str, &str),
   104	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   105	    if let Value::Array(vs) = v {
   106	        for v in vs {
   107	            transfer(tx, v, from, to)?;
   108	        }
   109	        return Ok(());
   110	    }
   111	    if v == &Value::Null {
   112	        return Ok(());
   113	    }
   114	    let id = crate::assets::asset_id(Some(v))
   115	        .ok_or_else(|| refuse("extraction asset has no typed ledger id"))?;
   116	    let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT bytes FROM _assets WHERE id=?1 AND home_table=?2 AND home_id=?3 AND home_column=?4 AND released_at IS NULL",params![id,from.0,from.1,from.2],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   117	    let bytes = bytes.ok_or_else(|| {
   118	        refuse(format!(
   119	            "extraction asset {id} has no live source home; never claim or duplicate it"
   120	        ))
   121	    })?;
   122	    let root = tx
   123	        .meta("activation_asset_root")?
   124	        .ok_or_else(|| refuse("extraction requires activation asset custody"))?;
   125	    if id.is_empty()
   126	        || !id
   127	            .bytes()
   128	            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_')
   129	    {
   130	        return Err(refuse("invalid extraction asset id"));
   131	    }
   132	    let path = std::path::Path::new(&root).join(id);
   133	    let meta = std::fs::symlink_metadata(&path)
   134	        .map_err(|_| refuse(format!("extraction asset {id} bytes are unavailable")))?;
   135	    if !meta.is_file() || meta.len() != bytes as u64 {
   136	        return Err(refuse(format!(
   137	            "extraction asset {id} byte length disagrees with its ledger"
   138	        )));
   139	    }
   140	    if from != to {
   141	    tx.conn().execute(
   142	        "UPDATE _assets SET home_table=?2,home_id=?3,home_column=?4 WHERE id=?1",
   143	        params![id, to.0, to.1, to.2],
   144	    )?;
   145	    }
   146	    Ok(())
   147	}
   148	pub(super) fn transition(
   149	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   150	    next: &Schema,
   151	    previous: Option<&Schema>,
   152	    sources: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
   153	    report: &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport,
   154	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   155	    if next.extractions.is_empty() && previous.is_none_or(|s| s.extractions.is_empty()) {
   156	        return Ok(());
   157	    }
   158	    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_rows(definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,child_t INTEGER NOT NULL,child_id TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(definition,parent_t,parent_id),UNIQUE(definition,child_t,child_id)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
   159	    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_cells(definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(definition,parent_t,parent_id,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
   160	    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _extraction_active(child_t INTEGER NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,parent_name TEXT NOT NULL,reference_column TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(child_t,definition)) WITHOUT ROWID; DELETE FROM _extraction_active;")?;
   161	    for e in &next.extractions {
   162	        tx.conn().execute(
   163	            "INSERT INTO _extraction_active VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4)",
   164	            params![tx.num(&e.child)?, key(tx,e)?, e.parent, e.reference],
   165	        )?;
   166	    }
   167	    tx.set_meta("version", super::table_renames::VERSION)?;
   168	    for e in previous
   169	        .into_iter()
   170	        .flat_map(|s| &s.extractions)
   171	        .filter(|e| !next.extractions.contains(e))
   172	    {
   173	        let Some(parent) = next.table(&e.parent) else {
   174	            continue;
   175	        };
   176	        let child = &previous.unwrap().tables[&e.child];
   177	        let pt = tx.num(&e.parent)?;
   178	        let ct = tx.num(&e.child)?;
   179	        let key = key(tx,e)?;
   180	        let mut count = 0;
   181	        let inherited = child
   182	            .through
   183	            .as_ref()
   184	            .is_some_and(|t| t.reference == e.reference && t.parent == e.parent);
   185	        let current_parent = &previous.unwrap().tables[&e.parent];
   186	        if parent.rules.read != crate::schema::RuleExpr::Deny
   187	            && ((inherited && parent.rules.read != current_parent.rules.read)
   188	                || (!inherited && parent.rules.read != child.rules.read))
   189	        {
   190	            return Err(refuse(
   191	                "reverse extraction would broaden the current asset audience; preserve current read policy in an authored bridge",
   192	            ));
   193	        }
   194	        for (dest, source) in &e.fields {
   195	            if asset(&child.columns[dest])
   196	                && parent.columns.contains_key(source)
   197	                && parent.column_reads.get(source) != child.column_reads.get(dest)
   198	            {
   199	                return Err(refuse(
   200	                    "reverse extraction would change the asset column audience; retain its current gate in a bridge",
   201	                ));
   202	            }
   203	        }
   204	        if e.each { each::backward(tx,e,parent,current_parent,child,&key,report)?; continue; }
   205	        super::activation::walk(tx, pt, |id, mut row| {
   206	            let saved:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT child_id FROM _extraction_rows WHERE definition=?1 AND parent_t=?2 AND parent_id=?3",params![key,pt,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   207	            let child_id = if let Some(id) = saved {
   208	                id
   209	            } else {
   210	                let mut found = Vec::new();
   211	                super::activation::walk(tx, ct, |child_id, child| {
   212	                    if child.field(&e.reference) == Ok(&Value::Id(id.into())) {
   213	                        if found.len() < 2 {
   214	                            found.push(child_id.to_owned());
   215	                        }
   216	                    }
   217	                    Ok(())
   218	                })?;
   219	                if found.len() > 1 {
   220	                    return Err(refuse(format!(
   221	                        "extraction {} parent {id}: several child rows compete for the old asset; declare a truthful bridge",
   222	                        e.child
   223	                    )));
   224	                }
   225	                let Some(child) = found.pop() else {
   226	                    return Ok(());
   227	                };
   228	                child
   229	            };
   230	            let Some(mut image) = load(tx, ct, &child_id)? else {
   231	                return Err(refuse(format!(
   232	                    "extraction child {}/{child_id} was deleted; old asset has no truthful representation",
   233	                    e.child
   234	                )));
   235	            };
   236	            if image.field(&e.reference) != Ok(&Value::Id(id.into())) {
   237	                return Err(refuse(
   238	                    "extraction child reference changed; refusing to rebind its parent lifetime",
   239	                ));
   240	            }
   241	            let anchored:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND parent_t=?4 AND parent_id=?5 AND invalid=0)",params![ct,child_id,format!("$extract:{key}"),pt,id],|r|r.get(0))?;
   242	            if !anchored {
   243	                return Err(refuse(
   244	                    "extraction child lacks valid parent-lifetime evidence; refusing deletion/id reuse or retrospective recapture",
   245	                ));
   246	            }
   247	            for (destination, source) in &e.fields {
   248	                let kind = &child.columns[destination];
   249	                if !asset(kind) {
   250	                    if row.field(source).ok() != image.field(destination).ok() {
   251	                        return Err(refuse(
   252	                            "extraction immutable scalar copies disagree with the current parent",
   253	                        ));
   254	                    }
   255	                    continue;
   256	                }
   257	                if !parent.columns.contains_key(source) {
   258	                    continue;
   259	                }
   260	                if parent.columns[source] != *kind {
   261	                    return Err(refuse("reverse extraction requires identical asset types"));
   262	                }
   263	                let v = image.field(destination)?.clone();
   264	                retain_cell(tx, &key, pt, id, source, &v)?;
   265	                transfer(
   266	                    tx,
   267	                    &v,
   268	                    (&e.child, &child_id, destination),
   269	                    (&e.parent, id, source),
   270	                )?;
   271	                fields(&mut row)?.insert(source.clone(), v);
   272	                fields(&mut image)?.remove(destination);
   273	                tx.conn().execute(
   274	                    "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
   275	                    params![pt, id, source],
   276	                )?;
   277	            }
   278	            write(tx, pt, id, &row)?;
   279	            write(tx, ct, &child_id, &image)?;
   280	            tx.conn().execute(
   281	                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _extraction_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",
   282	                params![key, pt, id, ct, child_id],
   283	            )?;
   284	            count += 1;
   285	            Ok(())
   286	        })?;
   287	        report.backfills.push(format!(
   288	            "extract {}: moved asset homes back for {count} parent rows",
   289	            e.child
   290	        ));
   291	    }
   292	    for e in next
   293	        .extractions
   294	        .iter()
   295	        .filter(|e| previous.is_none_or(|s| !s.extractions.contains(e)))
   296	    {
   297	        e.admit(next).map_err(refuse)?;
   298	        let parent = sources.get(&e.parent).unwrap_or(&next.tables[&e.parent]);
   299	        let child = &next.tables[&e.child];
   300	        let pt = tx.num(&e.parent)?;

     1	//! Typed one-child extraction; home transfer is an activation operation.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — an asset is moved with its one home.
     3	use super::*;
     4	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
     5	pub struct Extraction {
     6	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "std::ops::Not::not")]
     7	    pub each: bool,
     8	    pub child: String,
     9	    pub parent: String,
    10	    pub reference: String,
    11	    pub fields: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    12	}
    13	impl Extraction {
    14	    pub fn admit(&self, schema: &Schema) -> Result<(), String> {
    15	        if schema.extractions.iter().filter(|e|e.child==self.child).count()>1 {
    16	            return Err("one extraction declaration owns each child table; native children cannot be assigned to competing sources".into());
    17	        }
    18	        let child = schema
    19	            .table(&self.child)
    20	            .ok_or("extraction child is not declared")?;
    21	        if self.child == self.parent
    22	            || schema.table(&self.parent).is_none()
    23	            || child.ephemeral.is_some()
    24	            || schema.maintain_for(&self.child).is_some()
    25	        {
    26	            return Err("extraction needs distinct authoritative durable parent and child tables without captured ownership".into());
    27	        }
    28	        if child.columns.get(&self.reference)
    29	            != Some(&ColumnType::Ref {
    30	                table: self.parent.clone(),
    31	            })
    32	        {
    33	            return Err("extraction needs a required reference to its parent".into());
    34	        }
    35	        if !matches!(
    36	            child.on_delete.get(&self.reference),
    37	            Some(references::OnDelete::Cascade | references::OnDelete::Restrict)
    38	        ) {
    39	            return Err("reversible extraction requires on delete cascade or restrict; kept references cannot prove the parent lifetime".into());
    40	        }
    41	        if self.fields.is_empty()
    42	            || self.fields.contains_key(&self.reference)
    43	            || self.fields.keys().any(|c| {
    44	                !child.columns.contains_key(c)
    45	                    || c == "id"
    46	                    || child.engine_field(c)
    47	                    || child.maintained.contains_key(c)
    48	                    || child.sequences.contains_key(c)
    49	                    || child.written_by.contains_key(c)
    50	            })
    51	        {
    52	            return Err("extraction fields must be distinct authoritative child columns".into());
    53	        }
    54	        if self.each {
    55	            let assets:Vec<_>=self.fields.keys().filter(|c|asset(&child.columns[*c])).collect();
    56	            if assets.len()!=1 || !matches!(child.columns[assets[0]],ColumnType::Image{..}|ColumnType::File{..}|ColumnType::Video{..}) {
    57	                return Err("extract each requires exactly one required scalar image/file/video destination; map its historical bounded list".into());
    58	            }
    59	        }
    60	        if !self.fields.keys().any(|c| asset(&child.columns[c])) {
    61	            return Err("extraction needs at least one mapped image/file column".into());
    62	        }
    63	        if child.columns.iter().any(|(c, k)| {
    64	            c != &self.reference
    65	                && c != "id"
    66	                && !self.fields.contains_key(c)
    67	                && !k.is_optional()
    68	                && !child.defaults.contains_key(c)
    69	                && !child.engine_field(c)
    70	        }) {
    71	            return Err("map every required child column or give it an explicit default".into());
    72	        }
    73	        Ok(())
    74	    }
    75	}
    76	pub(super) fn asset(k: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    77	    match k {
    78	        ColumnType::Optional(k)
    79	        | ColumnType::List { of: k, .. }
    80	        | ColumnType::ListRange { of: k, .. } => asset(k),
    81	        ColumnType::Image { .. } | ColumnType::File { .. } | ColumnType::Video { .. } => true,
    82	        _ => false,
    83	    }
    84	}

   402	                };
   403	                if asset(kind) {
   404	                    if image.field(destination).is_ok_and(|old| old != &v) {
   405	                        return Err(refuse(
   406	                            "extraction destination asset conflicts with its source",
   407	                        ));
   408	                    }
   409	                    retain_cell(tx, &key, pt, id, source, &v)?;
   410	                    transfer(
   411	                        tx,
   412	                        &v,
   413	                        (&e.parent, id, source),
   414	                        (&e.child, &child_id, destination),
   415	                    )?;
   416	                    fields(&mut image)?.insert(destination.clone(), v);
   417	                    fields(&mut row)?.remove(source);
   418	                    tx.conn().execute(
   419	                        "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
   420	                        params![pt, id, source],
   421	                    )?;
   422	                } else if saved.is_none() {
   423	                    fields(&mut image)?.insert(destination.clone(), v);
   424	                } else if image.field(destination).ok() != Some(&v) {
   425	                    return Err(refuse(
   426	                        "extraction immutable scalar copies disagree with the returning parent",
   427	                    ));
   428	                }
   429	            }
   430	            crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, child);
   431	            write(tx, pt, id, &row)?;
   432	            write(tx, ct, &child_id, &image)?;
   433	            tx.conn().execute(
   434	                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _extraction_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",
   435	                params![key, pt, id, ct, child_id],
   436	            )?;
   437	            super::dormant::capture_refs(
   438	                tx,
   439	                ct,
   440	                &child_id,
   441	                &format!("$extract:{key}"),
   442	                &ColumnType::Ref {
   443	                    table: e.parent.clone(),
   444	                },
   445	                &Value::Id(id.into()),
   446	            )?;
   447	            count += 1;
   448	            Ok(())
   449	        })?;
   450	        report.backfills.push(format!(
   451	            "extract {}: prepared {count} child rows and moved existing asset homes",
   452	            e.child
   453	        ));
   454	    }
   455	    tx.set_meta("activation_population", "true")?;
   456	    Ok(())
   457	}
   458	pub(super) fn delete(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   459	    if exists(tx)? {
   460	        each::delete(tx,t,id)?;
   461	        tx.conn().execute(
   462	            "DELETE FROM _extraction_cells WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2",
   463	            params![t, id],
   464	        )?;
   465	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _extraction_rows WHERE (parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2) OR (child_t=?1 AND child_id=?2)",params![t,id])?;
   466	    }
   467	    Ok(())
   468	}
   469	
   470	pub(super) fn observe_write(
   471	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   472	    t: i64,
   473	    id: &str,
   474	    row: &Value,
   475	    old: Option<&Value>,
   476	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   477	    if !exists(tx)? {
   478	        return Ok(());
   479	    }
   480	    let active:Vec<(String,String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition,parent_name,reference_column FROM _extraction_active WHERE child_t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   481	    for (key, parent, column) in active {
   482	        let binding = format!("$extract:{key}");
   483	        let existing: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
   484	            "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",
   485	            params![t, id, binding],
   486	            |r| r.get(0),
   487	        )?;
   488	        if existing && old.is_some_and(|old| old.field(&column).ok() == row.field(&column).ok()) {
   489	            continue;
   490	        }
   491	        if let Ok(value) = row.field(&column) {
   492	            super::dormant::capture_refs(
   493	                tx,
   494	                t,
   495	                id,
   496	                &binding,
   497	                &ColumnType::Ref { table: parent },
   498	                value,
   499	            )?;
   500	        }
   501	    }
   502	    Ok(())
   503	}
   504	
   505	fn retain_cell(
   506	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   507	    key: &str,
   508	    t: i64,
   509	    id: &str,
   510	    column: &str,
   511	    value: &Value,
   512	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   513	    let old: Option<String> = tx
   514	        .conn()
   515	        .query_row(
   516	            "SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
   517	            params![t, id, column],
   518	            |r| r.get(0),
   519	        )
   520	        .optional()?;
   521	    if let Some(old) = old {
   522	        let old: Value = serde_json::from_str(&old).map_err(|e| refuse(e.to_string()))?;
   523	        if &old != value {
   524	            return Err(refuse(
   525	                "extraction has a distinct retained source cell; preserve both meanings in an authored bridge",
   526	            ));
   527	        }
   528	    }
   529	    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _extraction_cells VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5) ON CONFLICT(definition,parent_t,parent_id,column_name) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data",params![key,t,id,column,canonical_json(value)])?;
   530	    Ok(())
   531	}

     1	//! Private filesystem-owner control transport; never an HTTP application door.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — the host remains the only database writer.
     3	use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

    58	#[cfg(unix)]
    59	pub(crate) fn identity(store: &Path) -> Result<String, String> {
    60	    let path = store.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    61	    let m = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path)
    62	        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
    63	        .ok_or("store disappeared")?;
    64	    Ok(crate::native::digest(
    65	        &serde_json::to_vec(&(path.as_os_str().as_bytes(), m.dev(), m.ino())).unwrap(),
    66	    ))
    67	}

    69	#[cfg(unix)]
    70	fn peer(stream: &UnixStream) -> io::Result<()> {
    71	    use std::os::fd::AsRawFd;
    72	    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    73	    let uid = {
    74	        let mut credentials = std::mem::MaybeUninit::<libc::ucred>::uninit();
    75	        let mut size = std::mem::size_of::<libc::ucred>() as libc::socklen_t;
    76	        if unsafe {
    77	            libc::getsockopt(
    78	                stream.as_raw_fd(),
    79	                libc::SOL_SOCKET,
    80	                libc::SO_PEERCRED,
    81	                credentials.as_mut_ptr().cast(),
    82	                &mut size,
    83	            )
    84	        } != 0
    85	        {
    86	            return Err(io::Error::last_os_error());
    87	        }
    88	        if size as usize != std::mem::size_of::<libc::ucred>() {
    89	            return Err(io::Error::other("invalid peer credentials"));
    90	        }
    91	        unsafe { credentials.assume_init() }.uid
    92	    };
    93	    #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
    94	    let uid = {
    95	        let (mut uid, mut gid) = (0, 0);
    96	        if unsafe { libc::getpeereid(stream.as_raw_fd(), &mut uid, &mut gid) } != 0 {
    97	            return Err(io::Error::last_os_error());
    98	        }
    99	        uid
   100	    };
   101	    #[cfg(not(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "macos")))]
   102	    return Err(io::Error::other(
   103	        "release control peer credentials unsupported on this Unix host",
   104	    ));
   105	    #[cfg(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "macos"))]
   106	    if uid != unsafe { libc::geteuid() } {
   107	        return Err(io::Error::other(
   108	            "release control requires the same filesystem owner",
   109	        ));
   110	    }
   111	    Ok(())
   112	}
   113	
   114	#[cfg(unix)]
   115	fn socket_entry(path: &Path) -> io::Result<fs::Metadata> {
   116	    let m = fs::symlink_metadata(path)?;
   117	    if !m.file_type().is_socket()
   118	        || m.uid() != unsafe { libc::geteuid() }
   119	        || m.permissions().mode() & 0o7777 != 0o600
   120	    {
   121	        return Err(io::Error::other(
   122	            "release control must be a same-owner 0600 socket, never a symlink or regular file",
   123	        ));
   124	    }
   125	    Ok(m)
   126	}
   127	
   128	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#51-path-independent-control-address — fixed short runtime root, independent of shell environment.
   129	#[cfg(unix)]
   130	fn private_directory(path: &Path, create: bool) -> io::Result<()> {
   131	    if create {
   132	        match fs::DirBuilder::new().mode(0o700).create(path) {
   133	            Ok(()) => {}
   134	            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => {}
   135	            Err(e) => return Err(e),
   136	        }
   137	    }
   138	    // Do not chmod or follow an existing entry. A stranger can pre-create a
   139	    // name in /tmp, but cannot turn that into a capability or chmod target.
   140	    let directory = fs::OpenOptions::new()
   141	        .read(true)
   142	        .custom_flags(libc::O_DIRECTORY | libc::O_NOFOLLOW)
   143	        .open(path)?;
   144	    directory_metadata(&directory.metadata()?, unsafe { libc::geteuid() })
   145	}
   146	
   147	#[cfg(unix)]
   148	fn directory_metadata(m: &fs::Metadata, uid: u32) -> io::Result<()> {
   149	    if !m.is_dir() || m.uid() != uid || m.permissions().mode() & 0o7777 != 0o700 {
   150	        return Err(io::Error::other(
   151	            "release control runtime directory must be a same-owner 0700 directory",
   152	        ));
   153	    }
   154	    Ok(())
   155	}
   156	
   157	#[cfg(unix)]
   158	fn address(dir: &Path, create: bool) -> io::Result<PathBuf> {
   159	    let directory = PathBuf::from(format!("/tmp/snapback4-{}", unsafe { libc::geteuid() }));
   160	    private_directory(&directory, create)?;
   161	    let canonical = dir.canonicalize()?;
   162	    // Bind the address to immutable deployment source, even when two
   163	    // deployments symlink their database to the same store. The actual
   164	    // database path/inode is separately checked in every envelope.
   165	    Ok(directory.join(format!(
   166	        "{}.sock",
   167	        crate::native::digest(canonical.as_os_str().as_bytes())
   168	    )))
   169	}
   170	
   171	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#51-path-independent-control-address — a total input deadline bounds shutdown even when a peer keeps sending bytes.
   172	#[cfg(unix)]
   173	fn input(stream: &mut UnixStream, timeout: Duration) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
   174	    let deadline = Instant::now() + timeout;
   175	    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
   176	    let mut buffer = [0; INPUT + 1];
   177	    while bytes.len() <= INPUT {
   178	        let remaining = deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
   179	        if remaining.is_zero() {
   180	            return Err(io::Error::new(
   181	                io::ErrorKind::TimedOut,
   182	                "release control input deadline exceeded",
   183	            ));
   184	        }
   185	        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(remaining))?;
   186	        match stream.read(&mut buffer[..INPUT + 1 - bytes.len()]) {
   187	            Ok(0) => break,
   188	            Ok(n) => bytes.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..n]),
   189	            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
   190	            Err(e) => return Err(e),
   191	        }
   192	    }
   193	    Ok(bytes)
   194	}
   195	
   196	/// Only called while the exclusive database sidecar is held, so stale socket
   197	/// replacement cannot disconnect another admitted serving owner.
   198	#[cfg(unix)]
   199	pub(crate) struct Listener {
   200	    listener: UnixListener,
   201	    path: PathBuf,
   202	    inode: (u64, u64),
   203	    identity: String,
   204	}
   205	#[cfg(unix)]
   206	impl Listener {
   207	    pub(crate) fn bind(dir: &Path, store: &Path) -> Result<Self, String> {
   208	        let identity = identity(store)?;
   209	        let path = address(dir, true).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   210	        match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
   211	            Ok(_) => {
   212	                socket_entry(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   213	                match UnixStream::connect(&path) {
   214	                    Ok(_) => {
   215	                        return Err("release control socket already has a live listener".into());
   216	                    }
   217	                    Err(e)
   218	                        if matches!(
   219	                            e.kind(),
   220	                            io::ErrorKind::ConnectionRefused | io::ErrorKind::NotFound
   221	                        ) => {}
   222	                    Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
   223	                }
   224	                fs::remove_file(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   225	            }
   226	            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
   227	            Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
   228	        }
   229	        let listener = UnixListener::bind(&path)
   230	            .map_err(|e| format!("release control {}: {e}", path.display()))?;
   231	        let m = fs::symlink_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   232	        let owned = Self {
   233	            listener,
   234	            path,
   235	            inode: (m.dev(), m.ino()),
   236	            identity,
   237	        };
   238	        // Establish cleanup custody before any fallible setup after bind.
   239	        fs::set_permissions(&owned.path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600))
   240	            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   241	        owned
   242	            .listener
   243	            .set_nonblocking(true)
   244	            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   245	        socket_entry(&owned.path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

     8	#[cfg(unix)]
     9	pub(super) fn start(
    10	    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    11	    dir: std::path::PathBuf,
    12	) -> Result<std::thread::JoinHandle<()>, String> {
    13	    let store = shared
    14	        .owner

    16	        .ok_or("release control requires a persisted owner")?
    17	        .path()
    18	        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

    20	    crate::lifecycle::managed_cleanup();
    21	    crate::lifecycle::background("release-control", move || {
    22	        listener.run(|command| apply(&shared, &dir, command))

    26	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — only assignments and committed rebind follow the database commit.
    27	pub(super) fn apply(
    28	    shared: &Shared,
    29	    dir: &std::path::Path,
    30	    command: Command,
    31	) -> Result<Json, String> {
    32	    match command {
    33	        Command::Stop => {
    34	            crate::lifecycle::request_stop();
    35	            Ok(json!({"stopping":true}))
    36	        }
    37	        Command::Status {
    38	            after,
    39	            items_after,
    40	            event,
    41	        } => {
    42	            shared.engine.attempt(move |engine, shared| {
    43	            let mut status=engine.store_mut().release_status(after,items_after.as_deref(),event).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    44	            status["clock"]=json!({"now":shared.now(),"offsetMs":lock(&shared.clock).offset()});
    45	            Ok(status)
    46	            })
    47	        },
    48	        Command::Prune {
    49	            confirm,
    50	            expected_revision,
    51	        } => {
    52	            let mut result = shared.engine.attempt(move |engine, _| engine.store_mut().prune_dormant(confirm, Some(expected_revision)))
    53	                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    54	            if let Some(items) = result["items"].as_array_mut() {
    55	                items.truncate(16);
    56	            }
    57	            result["items_after"] = result["items"]
    58	                .as_array()
    59	                .and_then(|items| items.last())
    60	                .map(|i| i["key"].clone())
    61	                .unwrap_or(Json::Null);
    62	            Ok(result)
    63	        }
    64	        Command::Activate {
    65	            activation_protocol,
    66	            release,
    67	            expected_release,
    68	            expected_revision,
    69	            digest,
    70	            rollback,
    71	            visible_rollback,
    72	            registry_custody,
    73	            restore_dormant,
    74	            allow_feed_adoption,
    75	        } => {
    76	            if activation_protocol != crate::release_control::ACTIVATION_PROTOCOL {
    77	                return Err("release activation protocol differs; restart serve with this CLI before switching releases".into());
    78	            }
    79	            if release == 0 {
    80	                return Err("release id must be positive".into());
    81	            }
    82	            let project = Project {
    83	                root: dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string()),
    84	            };
    85	            if !std::fs::symlink_metadata(&project.root)
    86	                .is_ok_and(|m| m.is_dir() && !m.file_type().is_symlink())
    87	            {
    88	                return Err(format!(
    89	                    "release {release} is not an immutable release directory"
    90	                ));
    91	            }
    92	            if !registry_custody{return Err("release custody requires an updated release-control client; use this runtime's CLI".into())}
    93	            crate::verbs::validate_registered_view(dir,&project.root)?;
    94	            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
    95	                return Err("staged release content changed; redeploy its exact source".into());
    96	            }
    97	            if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
    98	                return Err(format!(
    99	                    "release {release} has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release"
   100	                ));
   101	            }
   102	            let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   103	            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
   104	                return Err(bound
   105	                    .diagnostics
   106	                    .iter()
   107	                    .map(ToString::to_string)
   108	                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
   109	                    .join("\n"));
   110	            }
   111	            let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   112	            crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
   113	            crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
   114	            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
   115	                return Err("release artifacts changed during preparation; retry after inspecting immutable source".into());
   116	            }
   117	            crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
   118	            let mut pointer =
   119	                crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, expected_release, rollback)?;
   120	            let options = ReleaseActivation {
   121	                visibility:crate::verbs::visibility_policy(&project.root)?.or_else(||visible_rollback.then(Default::default)),
   122	                release,
   123	                expected_previous: expected_release,
   124	                expected_revision: Some(expected_revision),
   125	                restore_dormant,
   126	                native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
   127	                operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
   128	            };
   129	            let _requests=shared.release_requests.write();
   130	            shared.engine.attempt(move |engine, shared| {
   131	            lock(&shared.readers).begin_adoption(&bound);
   132	            let generation = match engine.adopt_release_at(
   133	                bound.schema.clone(),
   134	                bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
   135	                &options,
   136	                allow_feed_adoption,
   137	                shared.now(),
   138	            ) {
   139	                Ok(generation) => generation,
   140	                Err(error) => {
   141	                    lock(&shared.readers).finish_adoption();
   142	                    return Err(error.to_string());
   143	                }
   144	            };

    41	export function deadline(ms: number | undefined, fallback: number): number {
    42	  const value = ms ?? fallback;
    43	  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 1) throw new RangeError("proof timeout must be positive and finite");

    46	/** @throws deadline expires; work rejection propagates. */
    47	export async function bounded<T>(work: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T> {
    48	  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    49	  try {
    50	    return await Promise.race([work, new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
    51	      timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(`${label} timed out after ${ms} ms`)), ms);
    52	    })]);
    53	  } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
    54	}
    55	/** Attempts every cleanup, even if a preceding step fails or stalls. */
    56	/** @throws AggregateError after every cleanup was attempted; each step has a 10-second bound. */
    57	export async function cleanup(...steps: Array<(() => unknown | Promise<unknown>) | undefined>): Promise<void> {
    58	  const errors: unknown[] = [];
    59	  for (const step of steps) if (step) {
    60	    try { await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(step), 10_000, "proof cleanup"); }
    61	    catch (error) { errors.push(error); }
    62	  }
    63	  if (errors.length) throw new AggregateError(errors, "proof cleanup failed");
    64	}
    65	/** @throws invalid handle/deadline, host stop failure or TERM/KILL reap deadline. */
    66	export async function stopChild(child: ChildProcess | Pick<ProofHost, "url" | "stop">, graceMs = 5000): Promise<void> {
    67	  deadline(graceMs, 5000);
    68	  if (child && "url" in child && typeof child.url === "string" && typeof child.stop === "function") {
    69	    await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(() => child.stop()), graceMs + 10_000, "host stop"); return;
    70	  }
    71	  if (!child || !("kill" in child) || typeof child.kill !== "function" || typeof child.once !== "function"
    72	      || !(child.exitCode === null || typeof child.exitCode === "number")
    73	      || !(child.signalCode === null || typeof child.signalCode === "string")) {
    74	    throw new TypeError("stopChild needs a ChildProcess or { url, stop }");
    75	  }
    76	  if (child.exitCode !== null || child.signalCode !== null) { await groupGone(child); return; }
    77	  let done!: () => void;
    78	  const exited = new Promise<void>(resolve => { done = resolve; });
    79	  child.once("exit", done);
    80	  child.once("error", done);
    81	  try {
    82	    child.kill("SIGTERM");
    83	    try { await bounded(exited, graceMs, "SIGTERM"); }
    84	    catch {
    85	      child.kill("SIGKILL");
    86	      await bounded(exited, 5000, "SIGKILL reap");
    87	    }
    88	  } finally { child.removeListener("exit", done); child.removeListener("error", done); }
    89	  await groupGone(child);
    90	}
    91	async function groupGone(child: ChildProcess): Promise<void> {
    92	  const pid = privateGroups.get(child);
    93	  if (!pid || process.platform === "win32") return;
    94	  const existing = groupClosures.get(child);
    95	  if (existing) return existing;
    96	  const cleanup = (async () => {
    97	    const expires = performance.now() + 5000;
    98	    for (;;) {
    99	      try { process.kill(-pid, 0); }
   100	      catch (error) { if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ESRCH") return; throw error; }
   101	      const remaining = expires - performance.now();
   102	      if (remaining <= 0) throw Error("proof process-group cleanup timed out after 5000 ms");
   103	      await sleep(Math.min(10, remaining));
   104	    }
   105	  })();
   106	  groupClosures.set(child, cleanup);
   107	  return cleanup;
   108	}

   110	export function portRange(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): string {
   111	  const low = env.PORT_LO, high = env.PORT_HI;
   112	  if (low === undefined && high === undefined) return "0";
   113	  if (!low || !high || !/^\d+$/.test(low) || !/^\d+$/.test(high)

   115	    throw Error("PORT_LO and PORT_HI must both specify an inclusive range, 1 <= low <= high <= 65535");
   116	  }
   117	  return `${Number(low)}-${Number(high)}`;
   118	}

   124	export function logged(command: string, args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}) {
   125	  const cwd = resolve(options.cwd ?? "."), id = randomUUID();
   126	  const directory = logsDir(options); mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
   127	  const logs = { stdout: join(directory, `${id}.stdout.log`), stderr: join(directory, `${id}.stderr.log`) };
   128	  if (options.secret) writeFileSync(logs.stdout, "stdout withheld: secret command (returned only to caller)\n", { flag: "wx", mode: 0o600 });
   129	  const out = options.secret ? undefined : openSync(logs.stdout, "wx", 0o600), err = openSync(logs.stderr, "wx", 0o600);
   130	  const grouped = options.processGroup === true && process.platform !== "win32";
   131	  // Frontends are already detached group leaders with an in-group keeper.
   132	  // Record their group too, so stop/startup cleanup cannot report success
   133	  // before Vite plugins and other inherited descendants have left custody.

   135	  const executable = grouped ? process.execPath : command;
   136	  const executableArgs = grouped ? [resolve(fileURLToPath(new URL("./proof-command.js", import.meta.url))), command, ...args] : [...args];

   138	  try { child = spawn(executable, executableArgs, { cwd, detached: grouped || (options.detached ?? false), env: { ...(options.env ?? process.env), SNAPBACK4_PROOF_LAUNCH: id }, stdio: [grouped || options.livenessPipe ? "pipe" : "ignore", options.secret ? "pipe" : out!, err, ...(grouped ? ["ipc" as const] : [])] }); }
   139	  finally { if (out !== undefined) closeSync(out); closeSync(err); }
   140	  if (privateGroup && child.pid) privateGroups.set(child, child.pid);
   141	  // Secret stdout never reaches a file, even briefly. Callers still receive it.
   142	  let stdout = "";
   143	  child.stdout?.setEncoding("utf8");
   144	  child.stdout?.on("data", chunk => { stdout += chunk; });
   145	  register(child);
   146	  child.stdin?.on("error", () => {});
   147	  let error: Error | undefined;
   148	  let actualPid = child.pid;
   149	  let actualCode: number | null = null;
   150	  let actualSignal: NodeJS.Signals | null = null;
   151	  let startedResolve!: () => void, startedReject!: (error: Error) => void, startedSettled = false;
   152	  const started = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => { startedResolve = resolve; startedReject = reject; });
   153	  void started.catch(() => {}); // command() observes close/error instead; host startup awaits this promise.
   154	  const didStart = () => { if (!startedSettled) { startedSettled = true; startedResolve(); } };
   155	  const failedStart = (value: unknown) => { if (!startedSettled) { startedSettled = true; startedReject(value instanceof Error ? value : Error(String(value))); } };
   156	  if (!grouped) didStart();
   157	  child.on("message", message => {
   158	    const event = message as {type?:string;pid?:number;code?:number|null;signal?:NodeJS.Signals|null;error?:string};
   159	    if (event.type === "started" && typeof event.pid === "number") { actualPid = event.pid; didStart(); }
   160	    if (event.type === "result") { actualCode = event.code ?? null; actualSignal = event.signal ?? null; if (event.error) error = Error(event.error); }
   161	  });
   162	  child.on("error", value => { error = value; failedStart(value); });
   163	  const closed = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => child.once("close", () => {
   164	    failedStart(error ?? Error("proof command exited before its process-group handshake"));
   165	    groupGone(child).then(resolve,reject);
   166	  }));
   167	  // Hosts use stopChild() as their cleanup join; commands await closed directly.
   168	  // Observe the promise here as well so an early frontend close cannot create
   169	  // an unhandled rejection before its startup catch reaches stopChild().
   170	  void closed.catch(() => {});
   171	  return { child, logs, id, closed, started, pid: () => actualPid, code: () => grouped ? actualCode : child.exitCode, signal: () => grouped ? actualSignal : child.signalCode, stdout: () => options.secret ? stdout : logText(logs.stdout), error: () => error };
   172	}
   173	export const logText = (path: string): string => readFileSync(path, "utf8");
   174	export async function ready(run: ReturnType<typeof logged>, parse: (line: string) => string | undefined, timeoutMs: number): Promise<string> {
   175	  const end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
   176	  let outAt = 0, errAt = 0;
   177	  while (performance.now() < end) {
   178	    if (run.error()) throw run.error();
   179	    if (run.child.exitCode !== null || run.child.signalCode !== null) {
   180	      throw Error(`proof child exited before ready (${run.child.exitCode ?? run.child.signalCode}); ${run.logs.stderr}\n${logText(run.logs.stderr).slice(-4000)}`);
   181	    }
   182	    for (const [path, offset, stream] of [[run.logs.stdout, outAt, "out"], [run.logs.stderr, errAt, "err"]] as const) {
   183	      const text = logText(path);
   184	      const complete = text.lastIndexOf("\n") + 1;
   185	      for (const line of text.slice(offset, complete).split("\n")) {
   186	        const url = parse(stripVTControlCharacters(line).trim());
   187	        if (url) return url;
   188	      }
   189	      if (stream === "out") outAt = complete; else errAt = complete;
   190	    }
   191	    await sleep(25);
   192	  }
   193	  throw Error(`proof child ready timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; logs: ${run.logs.stdout}, ${run.logs.stderr}`);
   194	}

     7	/// Conservative input-domain inclusion: every value accepted by old is accepted by new.
     8	pub fn accepts(old: &A, new: &A) -> bool {
     9	    accepts_in(old,new,None,None)
    10	}
    11	
    12	fn accepts_in(old: &A, new: &A, old_bound: Option<&Bound>, new_bound: Option<&Bound>) -> bool {
    13	    if old==new {return true;}
    14	    match (old,new) {
    15	        (A::Optional(a),A::Optional(b))=>accepts_in(a,b,old_bound,new_bound),
    16	        (A::Optional(_),_)=>false,
    17	        (a,A::Optional(b))=>accepts_in(a,b,old_bound,new_bound),
    18	        (A::Ref{table:old},A::Ref{table:new})=>new_bound.is_some_and(|bound|bound.schema.table_renames.get(new)==Some(old))
    19	            && old_bound.is_some_and(|bound|bound.schema.table(old).is_some()),
    20	        (A::Text{min:a,max:b,format:af},A::Text{min:c,max:d,format:bf})=>c<=a && d>=b && (bf.is_none() || af==bf),
    21	        (A::IntRange{..},A::Int)=>true,
    22	        (A::IntRange{min:a,max:b},A::IntRange{min:c,max:d})=>c<=a && d>=b,
    23	        (A::Enum{variants:a},A::Enum{variants:b})=>a.iter().all(|v|b.contains(v)),
    24	        (A::Bytes{max:a},A::Bytes{max:b}) | (A::Image{max:a},A::Image{max:b}) |
    25	        (A::Video{max:a},A::Video{max:b})=>b>=a,
    26	        (A::Json{max_bytes:a},A::Json{max_bytes:b})=>b>=a,
    27	        (A::File{max:a,types:at},A::File{max:b,types:bt})=>b>=a && snapback4_core::assets::FILE_TYPES.iter().all(|t|!snapback4_core::assets::file_accepts(at,t)||snapback4_core::assets::file_accepts(bt,t)),
    28	        (A::List{..}|A::ListRange{..},A::List{..}|A::ListRange{..})=> {
    29	            let parts=|t:&A|match t {A::List{of,max}=>(0,*max,of.clone()),A::ListRange{of,min,max}=>(*min,*max,of.clone()),_=>unreachable!()};
    30	            let (a,b,at)=parts(old);let(c,d,bt)=parts(new);
    31	            c<=a && d>=b && match(at,bt){(_,None)=>true,(Some(a),Some(b))=>accepts_in(&a,&b,old_bound,new_bound),_=>false}
    32	        }
    33	        _=>false,
    34	    }
    35	}
    36	
    37	#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
    38	enum ResultChange { Same, Additive, Changed }
    39	
    40	fn combine(fields: impl IntoIterator<Item=ResultChange>, extra: bool) -> ResultChange {
    41	    let mut additive=extra;
    42	    for field in fields {
    43	        match field {ResultChange::Changed=>return ResultChange::Changed,ResultChange::Additive=>additive=true,ResultChange::Same=>{}}
    44	    }
    45	    if additive {ResultChange::Additive} else {ResultChange::Same}
    46	}
    47	
    48	fn table_fields(name: &str, bound: &Bound) -> Option<BTreeMap<String,String>> {
    49	    let schema=bound.schema.public_projection();
    50	    let table=schema.tables.get(name)?;
    51	    Some(table.columns.iter().filter(|(n,_)|!table.engine_field(n)).map(|(n,t)|(n.clone(),format!("{t:?};private={};constraints={:?}",table.read_optional(n),table.constraints.iter().filter(|c|matches!(c,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Text{field,..} if field==n)).collect::<Vec<_>>()))).collect())
    52	}
    53	
    54	fn map_change<T>(old: &BTreeMap<String,T>, new: &BTreeMap<String,T>, compare: impl Fn(&T,&T)->ResultChange) -> ResultChange {
    55	    combine(old.iter().map(|(name,old)|new.get(name).map(|new|compare(old,new)).unwrap_or(ResultChange::Changed)),new.len()>old.len())
    56	}
    57	
    58	fn result_change(old: &Ty, new: &Ty, old_bound: &Bound, new_bound: &Bound) -> ResultChange {
    59	    if result(old,old_bound)==result(new,new_bound) {return ResultChange::Same;}
    60	    match (old,new) {
    61	        (Ty::Optional(a),Ty::Optional(b))|(Ty::Omittable(a),Ty::Omittable(b))
    62	        |(Ty::List(a,_),Ty::List(b,_))|(Ty::Collection(a,_),Ty::Collection(b,_))=>result_change(a,b,old_bound,new_bound),
    63	        (Ty::Object(a)|Ty::RowShape(a)|Ty::RowObject(a),Ty::Object(b)|Ty::RowShape(b)|Ty::RowObject(b))=>map_change(a,b,|a,b|result_change(a,b,old_bound,new_bound)),
    64	        (Ty::Row(a),Ty::Row(b)) | (Ty::WriteRow(a),Ty::WriteRow(b)) | (Ty::Page(a,_),Ty::Page(b,_)) if a==b=>{
    65	            let (Some(a),Some(b))=(table_fields(a,old_bound),table_fields(b,new_bound)) else{return ResultChange::Changed;};
    66	            combine(a.iter().map(|(name,kind)|if b.get(name)==Some(kind){ResultChange::Same}else{ResultChange::Changed}),b.len()>a.len())
    67	        }
    68	        _=>ResultChange::Changed,
    69	    }
    70	}
    71	
    72	fn action_result_change(old: Option<&BTreeMap<String,A>>, new: Option<&BTreeMap<String,A>>) -> ResultChange {
    73	    match (old,new) {
    74	        (None,None)=>ResultChange::Same,
    75	        (Some(old),Some(new))=>combine(old.iter().map(|(name,kind)|if new.get(name)==Some(kind){ResultChange::Same}else{ResultChange::Changed}),new.len()>old.len()),
    76	        _=>ResultChange::Changed,
    77	    }
    78	}
    79	
    80	pub fn is_note(line: &str) -> bool { line.contains(": RESULT NOTE:") }
    81	
    82	fn result(ty: &Ty, bound: &Bound) -> String {
    83	    match ty {
    84	        Ty::Row(name)|Ty::WriteRow(name)|Ty::Page(name,_)=> {
    85	            let schema=bound.schema.public_projection();
    86	            if let Some(table)=schema.tables.get(name) {
    87	                let fields: BTreeMap<_,_>=table.columns.iter().filter(|(n,_)|!table.engine_field(n)).map(|(n,t)|(n,format!("{t:?};private={};constraints={:?}",table.read_optional(n),table.constraints.iter().filter(|c|matches!(c,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Text{field,..} if field==n)).collect::<Vec<_>>()))).collect();
    88	                let kind=match ty {Ty::Row(..)=>"row",Ty::WriteRow(..)=>"write-row",Ty::Page(..)=>"page",_=>unreachable!()};
    89	                format!("{kind}:{name}:{fields:?}")
    90	            } else {format!("{ty:?}")}
    91	        }
    92	        Ty::Optional(t)=>format!("optional {}",result(t,bound)),
    93	        Ty::Omittable(t)=>format!("omittable {}",result(t,bound)),
    94	        Ty::List(t,_)|Ty::Collection(t,_)=>format!("list {}",result(t,bound)),
    95	        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>format!("object {:?}",fields.iter().map(|(n,t)|(n,result(t,bound))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>()),
    96	        Ty::Text(max)=>format!("text<={max}"),
    97	        _=>format!("{ty:?}"),
    98	    }
    99	}
   100	
   101	fn refusals(p: &BoundProgram) -> Vec<String> {
   102	    fn walk(v: &serde_json::Value, out: &mut Vec<String>) {
   103	        match v {
   104	            serde_json::Value::Object(o)=> {
   105	                if let Some(r)=o.get("Require") {out.push(format!("{}:{}",r["code"],r["message"]));}
   106	                for v in o.values(){walk(v,out);}
   107	            }
   108	            serde_json::Value::Array(a)=>for v in a{walk(v,out);},
   109	            _=>{}
   110	        }
   111	    }
   112	    let mut out=Vec::new();walk(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program.body).unwrap(),&mut out);out
   113	}
   114	
   115	/// Reports are advisory: compatibility cannot license calls or prove unchanged behavior.
   116	pub fn compare(old: &Bound, new: &Bound, label: &str) -> Vec<String> {
   117	    let mut lines=Vec::new();
   118	    for before in old.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Job) {
   119	        let name=&before.program.name;
   120	        let prefix=format!("old-client compatibility ({label}), operation {name}");
   121	        let Some(after)=new.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==*name) else {
   122	            lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: removed operation; old calls get E_OP; keep `alias {name} = replacement` for a same-module query/mutation, or retain a wrapper"));continue;
   123	        };
   124	        if before.program.kind!=after.program.kind {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: operation kind changed; retain the old endpoint kind"));continue;}
   125	        let rename_candidate = |new_kind: &A| before.program.args.iter().find(|(old_name, old_kind)|
   126	            !after.program.args.contains_key(*old_name) && !after.program.arg_aliases.contains_key(*old_name)
   127	                && accepts_in(old_kind,new_kind,Some(old),Some(new)));
   128	        let mut warned_renames=BTreeSet::new();
   129	        for (arg,kind) in &after.program.args {
   130	            if before.program.args.contains_key(arg) { continue; }
   131	            if let Some((old_arg,old_kind))=before.program.args.iter().find(|(old,_)|after.program.arg_aliases.get(*old)==Some(arg)) {
   132	                if !accepts_in(old_kind,kind,Some(old),Some(new)) {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: aliased argument {old_arg} → {arg} narrows the old input domain ({old_kind:?} → {kind:?}); retain the old input domain or a compatible wrapper"));}
   133	            } else if !matches!(kind,A::Optional(_)) {
   134	                if let Some((old,_))=rename_candidate(kind) {
   135	                    lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: required argument {old} appears renamed to {arg}; old calls get E_INPUT; declare `{arg}: <new type> alias {old}` if it is the same value, or retain a compatible wrapper"));
   136	                    warned_renames.insert(old.clone());
   137	                } else {
   138	                    lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: clients calling without new required argument {arg} get E_INPUT; give {arg} a fitting `default <literal>` or retain a compatible wrapper"));
   139	                }
   140	            }
   141	        }
   142	        for (arg,kind) in &before.program.args {
   143	            match after.program.args.get(arg) {
   144	                None if after.program.arg_aliases.contains_key(arg)=>{
   145	                    let next=&after.program.args[&after.program.arg_aliases[arg]];
   146	                    if !accepts_in(kind,next,Some(old),Some(new)){lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: aliased argument {arg} retyped or narrowed ({kind:?} → {next:?}); retain its old input domain"));}
   147	                },
   148	                None if warned_renames.contains(arg)=>{},
   149	                None=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: removed argument {arg}; old calls sending it get E_INPUT; retain `{arg}: <old type> deprecated` on a query/mutation")),
   150	                Some(next) if !accepts_in(kind,next,Some(old),Some(new))=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: argument {arg} retyped or narrowed ({kind:?} → {next:?}); old values may get E_INPUT; retain the old input domain or a compatible wrapper")),
   151	                _=>{}
   152	            }
   153	        }
   154	        for (alias,canonical) in &before.program.arg_aliases {
   155	            let old_kind=&before.program.args[canonical];
   156	            let next=after.program.args.get(alias).or_else(||after.program.arg_aliases.get(alias).and_then(|name|after.program.args.get(name)));
   157	            match next {
   158	                None=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: removed argument alias {alias}; old calls sending it get E_INPUT; retain its alias or a compatible wrapper")),
   159	                Some(kind) if !accepts_in(old_kind,kind,Some(old),Some(new))=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: argument alias {alias} retyped or narrowed ({old_kind:?} → {kind:?}); retain its old input domain")),
   160	                _=>{}
   161	            }
   162	        }
   163	        let mut positions:BTreeMap<String,String>=BTreeMap::new();
   164	        for canonical in before.program.args.keys() {
   165	            for spelling in std::iter::once(canonical).chain(before.program.arg_aliases.iter().filter_map(|(alias,target)|(target==canonical).then_some(alias))) {
   166	                let target=after.program.args.contains_key(spelling).then_some(spelling).or_else(||after.program.arg_aliases.get(spelling));
   167	                if let Some(target)=target {
   168	                    if let Some(prior)=positions.insert(target.clone(),canonical.clone())&&prior!=*canonical {
   169	                        lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: independent arguments {prior} and {canonical} now share {target}; old calls containing both positions get E_INPUT; retain separate arguments or a compatible wrapper"));
   170	                    }
   171	                }
   172	            }
   173	        }
   174	        for (table,before_table) in old.schema.visible_tables() {
   175	            let Some(after_table)=new.schema.table(table) else { continue; };
   176	            if !snapback4_core::ir::read_sites(&before.program).iter().any(|site|site.table==*table)
   177	                && !snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&before.program).iter().any(|(name,_)|name==table) { continue; }
   178	            for field in before_table.columns.keys().filter(|f|!before_table.read_optional(f)&&after_table.read_optional(f)) {
   179	                lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT REVIEW: {table}.{field} is now audience-optional; handle an omitted row property or a null explicit scalar before adopting the new column read rule"));
   180	            }
   181	        }
   182	        let action_change=action_result_change(old.schema.action_specs.get(name).and_then(|s|s.returns.as_ref()),new.schema.action_specs.get(name).and_then(|s|s.returns.as_ref()));
   183	        let typed_change=result_change(&before.result,&after.result,old,new);
   184	        let change=combine([action_change,typed_change],false);
   185	        match change {
   186	            ResultChange::Additive=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT NOTE: result shape added fields while preserving prior fields and domains; old clients may ignore the additions")),
   187	            ResultChange::Changed=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT REVIEW: result shape/value domain changed; preserve the old projection/domain or introduce a new operation before retiring old clients")),
   188	            ResultChange::Same=>{},
   189	        }
   190	        let old_domains=crate::result_domains::signature(before,old);
   191	        let new_domains=crate::result_domains::signature(after,new);
   192	        // A domain introduced only for a newly returned field is covered by
   193	        // the additive result note. Existing or removed domains still need review.
   194	        let changed:Vec<_>=old_domains.keys().collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().filter(|key|old_domains.get(*key)!=new_domains.get(*key)).cloned().collect();
   195	        if !changed.is_empty() {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT REVIEW: declared source domains consumed by this operation changed: {}; results or refusals may change even when the TypeScript type stays number/string; preserve the old domains or verify old-client handling",changed.join(", ")));}
   196	        if refusals(before)!=refusals(after) {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: BEHAVIOR REVIEW: refusal codes/messages changed ({:?} → {:?}); verify intended denials separately from input breaks",refusals(before),refusals(after)));}
   197	    }
   198	    lines
   199	}
   200	
   201	/// Human result advice groups source columns across operations. JSON keeps compare().
   202	pub fn compact(lines:&[String])->Vec<String>{
   203	    use std::collections::BTreeSet;
   204	    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=BTreeMap::<String,BTreeSet<String>>::new();
   205	    for line in lines {
   206	        let Some((prefix,detail))=line.split_once(": RESULT REVIEW: ") else {out.push(line.clone());continue};
   207	        let operation=prefix.rsplit_once("operation ").map(|(_,name)|name).unwrap_or("unknown");
   208	        if let Some((_,columns))=detail.split_once("declared source domains consumed by this operation changed: ") {
   209	            for column in columns.split(';').next().unwrap_or(columns).split(", "){groups.entry(column.into()).or_default().insert(operation.into());}
   210	        }else{groups.entry(detail.into()).or_default().insert(operation.into());}
   211	    }
   212	    for(detail,ops)in groups{out.push(format!("old-client compatibility: RESULT REVIEW: {detail}; operations [{}]; verify old-client handling (full per-operation evidence in --json)",ops.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")));}
   213	    out
   214	}

    13	/// The source stamp each `--no-watch` dev host adopted, by project root.
    14	static PINNED: Mutex<BTreeMap<PathBuf, SourceStamp>> = Mutex::new(BTreeMap::new());
    15	
    16	pub(super) fn closed() -> Refusal {
    17	    Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "development doors are closed; use serve staging with real users (guide staging), or explicitly opt into dev --unsafe-dev-doors for local fixtures").guide("staging")
    18	}

    20	pub(super) fn pin(project: &Path, stamp: SourceStamp) {
    21	    PINNED.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).insert(project.to_owned(), stamp);
    22	}

    24	/// A `--no-watch` host serves the schema it started with. When a request
    25	/// names an operation or argument that host does not know and the `.q` files
    26	/// changed on disk since, say so: the edit is real, it was just never adopted.
    27	pub(super) fn stale(shared: &Shared, refusal: Refusal) -> Refusal {
    28	    let Some(adopted) = PINNED.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).get(&shared.project).copied() else { return refusal };
    29	    if (Project { root: shared.project.clone() }).sources_stamp() == adopted { return refusal; }
    30	    let message = format!("{}; this dev host runs with --no-watch and snapback/ changed on disk after it adopted its schema, so the edit is not served", refusal.message);
    31	    Refusal { message, rewrite: Some("restart `snapback4 dev` (or drop --no-watch so saves re-adopt), then retry".into()), ..refusal }
    32	}

    34	// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — persona names are not their principals; dev only.
    35	pub(super) fn persona_argument(shared: &Shared, kinds: &BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::ir::ArgType>, args: &Json) -> Option<Refusal> {
    36	    if !shared.personas { return None; }

    40	// Rows are written as the system principal; each names its id because seed is an upsert by id.
    41	pub(super) fn seed(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    42	    if !shared.dev_doors() || !request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|a| a.ip().is_loopback()) {
    43	        respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "seeding is a development door")) }));
    44	        return;
    45	    }
    46	    let refuse = |request, refusal: Refusal| respond_timed(request, shared, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal.guide("personas")) }));
    47	    let rows: Vec<Json> = match serde_json::from_str(body) {
    48	        Ok(Json::Array(rows)) => rows,
    49	        _ => return refuse(request, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "seed takes a JSON array of {table, row}")),
    50	    };
    51	    let outcome = shared.engine.attempt(move |engine, shared| {
    52	        shared.dev_authority(engine).map_err(|why| (403, why))?;
    53	        let now = shared.now();
    54	        let typed = typed_rows(engine.schema(), &rows, now).map_err(|why| (400, why.guide("personas")))?;
    55	        let count = engine.seed_at(typed, now).map_err(|error| (200, seed_refusal(error)))?;
    56	        shared.durable_committed(engine);
    57	        Ok::<_, (u16, Refusal)>(count)
    58	    });
    59	    match outcome {
    60	        Ok(count) => respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"seeded":count})),
    61	        Err((status, why)) => respond_timed(request, shared, status, json!({"denied":why})),
    62	    }
    63	
    64	}
    65	
    66	/// `/seed` rows checked against the served schema and typed, or the first problem.
    67	fn typed_rows(schema: &snapback4_core::Schema, rows: &[Json], now: i64) -> Result<Vec<(String, snapback4_core::Value)>, Refusal> {
    68	    let mut typed = Vec::new();
    69	    for (index, entry) in rows.iter().enumerate() {
    70	        let (Some(table), Some(row)) = (entry.get("table").and_then(Json::as_str), entry.get("row")) else {
    71	            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "each seed entry is {table, row}"));
    72	        };
    73	        let Some(definition) = schema.table(table) else {
    74	            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("unknown table {table}")));
    75	        };
    76	        if row.is_object() && row.get("id").is_none_or(|id| !id.is_string()) {
    77	            return Err(missing_id(index, table));
    78	        }
    79	        let mut row = row.clone();
    80	        crate::verbs::test_fixture::relative_times(definition, &mut row, now)
    81	            .map_err(|why| Refusal::new("E_FIXTURE_TIME", "input", format!("seed row {index} ({table}): {why}")))?;
    82	        if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &row) {
    83	            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("seed row {index} ({table}): {why}")));
    84	        }
    85	        match wire::row_from_json(definition, &row) {
    86	            Ok(value) => typed.push((table.to_owned(), value)),
    87	            Err(why) => return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{table}: {why}"))),
    88	        }
    89	    }
    90	    Ok(typed)
    91	}
    92	
    93	fn seed_refusal(error: snapback4_core::RuntimeError) -> Refusal {
    94	    let mut refusal = error.refusal();
    95	    // Reseeding with changed immutable values reads like a rule problem; say what happened.
    96	    if refusal.code == "E_CONSTRAINT" && refusal.message.ends_with(" is immutable") {
    97	        refusal.message.push_str("; /seed is an upsert by id, and a row with that id already exists with a different value in that column. Reseed it with its original value, give the row a new id, POST /dev/reset first, or restart dev with --memory for an empty store");
    98	    }
    99	    refusal
   100	}
   101	
   102	/// What `POST /dev/reset` was asked to do: the body is empty, a `/seed`
   103	/// array, or `{"seed": [..], "clock": "reset"}` with either key optional.
   104	#[derive(Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
   105	struct ResetRequest { seed: Vec<Json>, clock: bool }
   106	
   107	fn reset_request(body: &str) -> Result<ResetRequest, Refusal> {
   108	    const SHAPE: &str = "reset takes no body, a /seed array [{table, row}, ..], or {\"seed\": [..], \"clock\": \"reset\"} with either key optional";
   109	    if body.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(ResetRequest::default()); }
   110	    match serde_json::from_str(body) {
   111	        Ok(Json::Array(seed)) => Ok(ResetRequest { seed, clock: false }),
   112	        Ok(Json::Object(fields)) => {
   113	            let mut request = ResetRequest::default();
   114	            for (key, value) in fields {
   115	                match (key.as_str(), value) {
   116	                    ("seed", Json::Array(seed)) => request.seed = seed,
   117	                    ("clock", Json::String(clock)) if clock == "reset" => request.clock = true,
   118	                    ("clock", Json::String(clock)) if clock == "keep" => request.clock = false,
   119	                    ("clock", other) => return Err(crate::dev_clock::input(format!("reset's clock is \"reset\" (back to wall time) or \"keep\" (the default), not {other}"))),
   120	                    (key, _) => return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{SHAPE}; got key {key:?}"))),
   121	                }
   122	            }
   123	            Ok(request)
   124	        }
   125	        _ => Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", SHAPE)),
   126	    }
   127	}
   128	
   129	// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.25 — one transaction: empty, new identity, reseed; then the host's caches follow.
   130	pub(super) fn reset(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
   131	    if !shared.dev_doors() || !request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|a| a.ip().is_loopback()) {
   132	        respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&reset_release()) }));
   133	        return;
   134	    }
   135	    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   136	    let refuse = |request, refusal: Refusal| respond_timed(request, shared, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal.guide("testing")) }));
   137	    let asked = match reset_request(body) {
   138	        Ok(asked) => asked,
   139	        Err(refusal) => return refuse(request, refusal),
   140	    };
   141	    // Serialize with clock advances, then take the engine before the clock:
   142	    // the order clock advance and worker claims use.
   143	    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
   144	    let _advance = lock(&advance);
   145	    let result = shared.engine.attempt(move |engine, shared| {
   146	
   147	    shared.dev_authority(engine).map_err(|why| (403, why))?;
   148	    let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock).clone();
   149	    if asked.clock { clock.reset().map_err(|why| (400, why))?; }
   150	    let now = clock.now();
   151	    // Resolve against the prospective reset clock before changing any durable state.
   152	    let typed = typed_rows(engine.schema(), &asked.seed, now).map_err(|why| (400, why))?;
   153	    let reset = engine.dev_reset_at(typed, now).map_err(|error| (200, seed_refusal(error)))?;
   154	    if asked.clock { *lock(&shared.clock) = clock; }
   155	    *lock(&shared.dev_reset_store) = Some(reset.store_id.clone());
   156	    // The reset committed a new identity and a new authority clock. Replace,
   157	    // rather than max with, the retired store's cached high-water mark.
   158	    shared.time_audience_clock.store(if engine.schema().tables.values().any(|t| t.not_before.is_some()) { now } else { i64::MIN }, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Release);
   159	    // Detached readers reopen; the following registry, change answers and
   160	    // `/dev/status` forget the old identity. Wake then names the new store,
   161	    // so every held poll returns and each client sees the replacement.
   162	    let sites = lock(&shared.bound).refusal_sites.clone();
   163	    lock(&shared.readers).replace(engine, &sites);
   164	    lock(&shared.adoption).store_id = reset.store_id.clone();
   165	    if let Ok(reader) = snapback4_core::engine::following_history::Reader::new() { *lock(&shared.following) = reader; }
   166	    *lock(&shared.change_answers) = Default::default();
   167	    shared.durable_committed(engine);
   168	    let generation = engine.generation();
   169	    Ok::<_, (u16, Refusal)>((reset, generation))
   170	    });
   171	    let (reset, generation) = match result {
   172	        Ok(value) => value,
   173	        Err((status, why)) => return respond_timed(request, shared, status, json!({"denied":why})),
   174	    };
   175	    let assets = reset.assets.len();
   176	    if let Err(error) = reset.assets.remove_files() { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: reset left asset bytes behind: {error}"); }
   177	    let now = shared.now();
   178	    snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4 dev: store reset (store {} → {}, seeded {}) in {} ms", reset.previous_store_id, reset.store_id, reset.seeded, started.elapsed().as_millis());
   179	    respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({
   180	        "sessionNotice":"Reset replaced the store and removed its accounts and sessions. Discard cached credentials/localStorage for this backend, sign up again and reprovision roles; use a fresh memory host per independent proof.",
   181	        "reset": true, "seeded": reset.seeded, "generation": generation, "storeId": reset.store_id,
   182	        "previousStoreId": reset.previous_store_id, "head": reset.seq, "assetsRemoved": assets,
   183	        "now": now, "clockOffsetMs": lock(&shared.clock).offset(), "skewMs": now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "ms": started.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,
   184	    }));
   185	}
   186	
   187	pub(super) fn reset_release() -> Refusal {
   188	    Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "resetting the store is a development door; snapback4 serve never empties a store")
   189	        .guide("testing")
   190	}
   191	
   192	fn missing_id(index: usize, table: &str) -> Refusal {
   193	    Refusal::new("E_FIELD", "input", format!(
   194	        "missing field id: seed row {index} ({table}) has no string id. /seed is an upsert by id, so every row names its own; \
   195	         rerunning the same seed then updates those rows instead of duplicating them"))
   196	        .rewrite("give each row a 26-character id in Crockford base32 (digits and capital letters except I, L, O, U), \
   197	                  e.g. \"row\": {\"id\": \"0000000000000000000000SEED\", ..}; in JavaScript `idFrom(\"seed:alice\")` from \
   198	                  snapback4/client derives the same valid id from a label every time, and a reference column takes that id")
   199	}

    12	pub fn input(message: impl Into<String>) -> Refusal { Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INPUT", "clock", message).guide("clock") }

    19	fn isolated_range(lo: Option<&str>, hi: Option<&str>) -> String {
    20	    match (lo.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok()), hi.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())) {
    21	        (Some(lo), Some(hi)) if lo > 0 && hi >= lo => format!("dev --memory --port {lo}-{hi} within the current range"),
    22	        _ => "dev --memory --port 0".into(),
    23	    }
    24	}
