    26	pub fn duration(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    27	    if text.starts_with('-') { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
    28	    let text = text.strip_prefix('+').unwrap_or(text);
    29	    let split = text.bytes().position(|b| !b.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(text.len());
    30	    let (number, unit) = text.split_at(split);
    31	    let multiplier = match unit { "ms" => 1, "s" => 1_000, "m" => 60_000, "h" => 3_600_000, "d" => 86_400_000, "w" => 604_800_000, _ => 0 };
    32	    number.parse::<i64>().ok().and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(multiplier)).filter(|n| *n > 0 && *n <= MAX_TIME)
    33	        .ok_or_else(|| input("expected a positive whole duration with ms, s, m, h, d or w, such as +25h"))
    34	}
    35	
    36	pub fn offset(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    37	    if let Some(rest) = text.strip_prefix('-') { duration(rest).map(|n| -n) } else { duration(text) }
    38	}
    39	
    40	fn time(value: Option<i64>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    41	    value.filter(|n| (0..=MAX_TIME).contains(n)).ok_or_else(|| input("time must be Unix milliseconds in 0..9007199254740991; choose a smaller time or duration"))
    42	}

    44	pub fn run_now(text: Option<&String>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    45	    let now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    46	    match text {
    47	        None => Ok(now),
    48	        Some(text) if text.starts_with('+') => time(now.checked_add(duration(text)?)),
    49	        Some(text) => time(text.parse().ok()),
    50	    }
    51	}

    53	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
    54	pub struct Clock {
    55	    dev: bool,
    56	    base: i64,
    57	    offset: i64,
    58	    started: Instant,
    59	    epoch: String,
    60	    revision: u64,
    61	    #[cfg(test)]
    62	    frozen: bool,
    63	}
    64	
    65	impl Clock {
    66	    pub fn new(dev: bool, offset: i64) -> Result<Self, Refusal> {
    67	        if !dev && offset != 0 { return Err(release()); }
    68	        Ok(Self { dev, offset, base: time(snapback4_core::now_ms().checked_add(offset))?, started: Instant::now(),
    69	            epoch: snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?, revision: 0, #[cfg(test)] frozen: false })
    70	    }
    71	    pub fn staging(offset: i64, floor: i64) -> Result<Self, Refusal> {
    72	        let mut clock = Self::new(true, offset)?;
    73	        let delta = floor.saturating_sub(clock.now()).max(0);
    74	        if delta > 0 { clock.advance(delta)?; }
    75	        Ok(clock)
    76	    }
    77	    #[cfg(test)]
    78	    pub(crate) fn freeze(&mut self, now: i64) { self.base = now; self.frozen = true; }
    79	    pub fn now(&self) -> i64 {
    80	        #[cfg(test)] if self.frozen { return self.base; }
    81	        if !self.dev { return snapback4_core::now_ms(); }
    82	        self.base.saturating_add(self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64).min(MAX_TIME)
    83	    }
    84	    /// Identifies samples taken before/after a restart or deliberate clock jump.
    85	    pub fn offset(&self) -> i64 { self.offset }
    86	    pub fn revision(&self) -> String { format!("{}:{}", self.epoch, self.revision) }
    87	    pub fn presence_now(&self) -> i64 {
    88	        // Production grace is elapsed real time, unaffected by wall-clock corrections.
    89	        if self.dev { self.now() } else { self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64 }
    90	    }
    91	    /// Back to wall time: the offset `--clock-offset` and every advance added
    92	    /// is dropped (a development reset's `{"clock":"reset"}`).
    93	    pub fn reset(&mut self) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    94	        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
    95	        self.revision += 1;
    96	        self.offset = 0;
    97	        self.base = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    98	        self.started = Instant::now();
    99	        #[cfg(test)] { self.frozen = false; }
   100	        Ok(self.base)
   101	    }
   102	    pub fn advance(&mut self, milliseconds: i64) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
   103	        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
   104	        if milliseconds <= 0 { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
   105	        let next = time(self.now().checked_add(milliseconds))?;
   106	        let offset = self.offset.checked_add(milliseconds).ok_or_else(|| input("cumulative clock offset overflow"))?;
   107	        self.revision += 1;
   108	        self.offset = offset;
   109	        self.base = next;
   110	        self.started = Instant::now();
   111	        Ok(next)
   112	    }
   113	}

   115	/// The control CLI only talks to a loopback dev host. It does not open its store.
   116	pub fn command(options: &crate::verbs::Options) -> i32 {
   117	    let result = (|| -> Result<String, String> {
   118	        let draining = options.positionals == ["drain"];
   119	        if !draining && (options.positionals.len() != 2 || options.positionals[0] != "advance") {

   130	            let session = crate::serve::token::mint_live(path, "clock", 1).map_err(|(_,error)|error)?;
   131	            (address,None,Some(session.token))
   132	        } else {
   133	            let (address,instance)=crate::verbs::dev_address(options)?;
   134	            // A production listener has no development-token registry entry.
   135	            // Ask its read-only clock door first so the CLI preserves the
   136	            // server's actionable refusal instead of failing token discovery.
   137	            if let Ok((_, status)) = crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange(address, "GET", "/dev/clock/status", None, "", Duration::from_secs(5))
   138	                && status["denied"]["code"] == "E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY"
   139	            {
   140	                return Err(release().to_string());
   141	            }
   142	            let token=crate::verbs::mint_for_dev(address,"operator",1)?;
   143	            (address,instance,Some(token))
   144	        };
   145	        let mut body = serde_json::json!({"duration":duration_text,"eachBoundary":options.flags.contains_key("each-boundary"), "drain":!options.flags.contains_key("no-drain"), "allowHeld":options.flags.contains_key("allow-held")});
   146	        if options.flags.contains_key("expire-sessions") { body["sessions"] = "expire".into(); }
   147	        if draining && options.flags.contains_key("no-drain") { return Err(input("clock drain does not accept --no-drain").to_string()); }
   148	        let body = body.to_string();
   149	        // The common HTTP/1.0 exchange is EOF-delimited and bounds replies at
   150	        // 64 MiB, enough for the preflight's complete explicit window report.
   151	        let response = if release_path.is_some() {
   152	            crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_authorized(address,"POST","/dev/clock/advance",None,token.as_deref(),&body,Duration::from_secs(60))
   153	        } else {
   154	            crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_dev_authorized(address,"/dev/clock/advance",instance.as_deref(),token.as_deref().unwrap(),&body,Duration::from_secs(60))
   155	        };
   156	        let (status, json) = response.map_err(|error| {
   157	            let evidence = crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange(address, "GET", "/dev/status", None, "", Duration::from_secs(5)).ok()
   158	                .map(|(_, status)| format!("now={}, skewMs={}", status["now"], status["skewMs"]))
   159	                .unwrap_or_else(|| "clock status unavailable".into());
   160	            format!("clock advance reply failed at {address}: {error}; time may already have advanced ({evidence}); inspect /dev/status and queued work before retrying; for an isolated dev pass --port N")
   161	        })?;
   162	        if let Some(notice) = json["sessionNotice"].as_str() { eprintln!("clock: {notice}"); }
   163	        if let Some(held) = json["held"].as_array() {
   164	            for schedule in held.iter().filter(|s| s["holdCode"] == "saved-boundary") {
   165	                eprintln!("clock: schedule {}: {}", schedule["name"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), schedule["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("held"));
   166	            }
   167	        }
   168	        if let Some(denied) = json.get("denied") {
   169	            if json.get("now").is_some() {
   170	                eprintln!("clock: incomplete advance report (time already moved): {}", serde_json::json!({"now":json["now"],"fired":json["fired"],"held":json["held"]}));
   171	            }
   172	            let clock = json["message"].as_str().map(|message| format!("; time remains advanced: {message}")).unwrap_or_default();
   173	            return Err(format!("{}: {}{clock}\n  guide: clock", denied["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("E_CLOCK_INPUT"), denied["message"].as_str().unwrap_or("clock advance failed")));
   174	        }
   175	        if status != 200 { return Err(format!("dev refused clock advance: {json}")); }
   176	        Ok(json.to_string())
   177	    })();
   178	    match result { Ok(body) => { println!("{body}"); 0 }, Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); 1 } }
   179	}

    45	async function host(port: number) {

    50	    const server = await createVite({ root, server: { host: "127.0.0.1", port, strictPort: true },
    51	      plugins: [{ name: "snapback4-proof-identity", configureServer(server: any) {
    52	        server.middlewares.use((_req: any, res: any, next: () => void) => { res.setHeader("x-snapback4-proof", marker); next(); });
    53	      } }] });
    54	    try { await server.listen(); }
    55	    catch (error) { await server.close(); throw error; }
    56	    const address = server.httpServer.address();

    58	    return { port: address.port, stop: () => server.close() };
    59	  }
    60	  const server = createServer((req, res) => {
    61	    res.setHeader("x-snapback4-proof", marker);

    70	    } catch {
    71	      // Extensionless app routes share the generic shell. Missing assets 404.
    72	      if (extname(file)) { res.writeHead(404).end(); return; }
    73	      file = join(root, "index.html");
    74	    }
    75	    try {

    77	      if (!inside(file) || !statSync(file).isFile()) { res.writeHead(403).end(); return; }
    78	      res.setHeader("content-type", mime[extname(file)] ?? "application/octet-stream");
    79	      if (req.method === "HEAD") { res.end(); return; }
    80	      const stream = createReadStream(file); stream.on("error", () => res.destroy()); stream.pipe(res);
    81	    } catch { res.writeHead(404).end(); }
    82	  });
    83	  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => { server.once("error", reject); server.listen(port, "127.0.0.1", () => { server.removeListener("error", reject); resolve(); }); });
    84	  const address = server.address();
    85	  if (!address || typeof address === "string") throw Error("static server has no bound TCP listener");
    86	  return { port: address.port, stop: () => new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    87	    server.close(error => error ? reject(error) : resolve()); server.closeAllConnections();
    88	  }) };
    89	}
    90	let started;
    91	for (let port = range[0]!; port <= range[1]!; port++) {
    92	  try { started = await host(port); break; }
    93	  catch (error) {
    94	    if (!/EADDRINUSE|Port \d+ is already in use/.test(String(error))) throw error;
    95	  }
    96	}
    97	if (!started) throw Error(`E_ADDRINUSE: every frontend port in ${ports} is in use`);

   101	  const timer = setTimeout(() => process.exit(1), 4000);
   102	  stop().then(() => { clearTimeout(timer); process.exit(0); }, () => process.exit(1));

    14	fn refusal_status(why: &snapback4_core::Refusal, fallback: u16) -> u16 {

    18	// Charge consumed CPU for pacing, not time the OS already kept this thread off
    19	// CPU. The cooperative chunk deadline still uses wall time. A failed clock
    20	// sample falls back to the more conservative wall-time pause.
    21	struct SliceClock { wall: Instant, cpu: Option<Duration> }
    22	impl SliceClock {
    23	    fn start() -> Self { Self { wall: Instant::now(), cpu: Self::cpu_time() } }
    24	    fn cpu_time() -> Option<Duration> {
    25	        let mut at = libc::timespec { tv_sec: 0, tv_nsec: 0 };
    26	        if unsafe { libc::clock_gettime(libc::CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID, &mut at) } != 0 { return None; }
    27	        Some(Duration::new(at.tv_sec.try_into().ok()?, at.tv_nsec.try_into().ok()?))
    28	    }
    29	    fn consumed(&self) -> Duration {
    30	        self.cpu.zip(Self::cpu_time()).and_then(|(start, end)| end.checked_sub(start)).unwrap_or_else(|| self.wall.elapsed())
    31	    }
    32	}
    33	
    34	// Detached readers do not take engine tickets. Sample OS load as well as the
    35	// round-3 engine contention signal; cache the syscall for 100 ms. Load includes
    36	// other tenants and trails short bursts, so this is a pacing hint, not admission.
    37	fn host_contended() -> bool {
    38	    use std::sync::{Mutex, OnceLock};
    39	    static SAMPLE: Mutex<(Option<Instant>, bool)> = Mutex::new((None, false));
    40	    static CPUS: OnceLock<usize> = OnceLock::new();
    41	    let mut sample = lock(&SAMPLE);
    42	    if sample.0.is_none_or(|at| at.elapsed() >= Duration::from_millis(100)) {
    43	        let mut load = 0.0;
    44	        let cpus = *CPUS.get_or_init(|| std::thread::available_parallelism().map_or(1, |n| n.get()));
    45	        sample.1 = unsafe { libc::getloadavg(&mut load, 1) } == 1 && load >= cpus as f64;
    46	        sample.0 = Some(Instant::now());
    47	    }
    48	    sample.1
    49	}

    51	fn engine_work<T: super::committer::QueueResult + Send + 'static>(shared: &Shared, run: impl FnOnce(&mut snapback4_core::Engine, Duration, &Shared) -> T + Send + 'static) -> T {
    52	    shared.engine.background_call(move |engine, shared, contended| {
    53	        lock(&shared.readers).retry_missing(engine);
    54	        let budget = if contended || host_contended() { CONTENDED_TIME } else { WORK_TIME };
    55	        let result = run(engine, budget, shared);
    56	        shared.publish_engine(engine);
    57	        result
    58	    })
    59	}
    60	
    61	fn phase<T: Default>(shared: &Shared, result: Result<T, RuntimeError>, drain: bool, name: &str) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
    62	    match result {
    63	        Err(error) if !drain => { super::adoption::background_failure(shared, name, &error); Ok(T::default()) }
    64	        other => other,
    65	    }
    66	}

    68	// @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — lifetimes are swept into the partition log; missed schedule boundaries fire once.
    69	fn work(shared: &Shared, drain: bool, tick: u64) -> Result<Work, RuntimeError> {
    70	    work_at(shared, drain, tick, None)
    71	}
    72	
    73	fn work_at(shared: &Shared, drain: bool, tick: u64, schedule_sample: Option<i64>) -> Result<Work, RuntimeError> {
    74	    let mut work = Work::default();
    75	    shared.ephemeral.sweep(shared.now())?;
    76	    work.delivery_chunks = delivery(shared, drain)?;
    77	    loop {
    78	        if phase(shared, engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| engine.run_view_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget)), drain, "view recount")?.is_none() {break;}
    79	        work.chunks += 1;
    80	        if !drain && work.chunks >= 500 {break;}
    81	        std::thread::yield_now();
    82	    }
    83	    loop {
    84	        let swept = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| engine.sweep_expired_for(shared.now(), 2_000, budget)), drain, "the expiry sweep")?;
    85	        work.expired += swept;
    86	        if !drain || swept == 0 { break; }
    87	    }
    88	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — advancing a replay clock does not release its schedule hold.
    89	    if !shared.hold_schedules {
    90	        let fired = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, move |engine, _, shared| {
    91	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.2 — select advance windows before draining; admission deadlines still use the live advanced clock.
    92	            if let Some(sample) = schedule_sample {
    93	                let windows = engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().map(|(index, schedule)| (index, schedule.boundary(sample))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    94	                let mut fired = Vec::new();
    95	                for (index, boundary) in windows { fired.extend(engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(shared.now(), index, boundary)?); }
    96	                Ok(fired)
    97	            } else { engine.fire_schedules(shared.now()) }
    98	        }), drain, "schedules")?;
    99	        work.schedules = fired.len();
   100	        for name in &fired { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: schedule {name} fired"); }
   101	        work.fired = fired;
   102	    }
   103	    while drain || work.chunks < 500 {
   104	        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { break; }
   105	        let (run, logs) = engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| {
   106	            let run = engine.run_job_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget);
   107	            (run, engine.take_native_logs())
   108	        });
   109	        for line in logs { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: native {line}"); }
   110	        let Some(run) = phase(shared, run, drain, "job")? else { break; };
   111	        work.chunks += 1;
   112	        match run.state {
   113	            snapback4_core::JobState::Done => snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} done: {} chunk{}, {} row{} written", run.name, run.chunks, if run.chunks == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, run.rows_written, if run.rows_written == 1 { "" } else { "s" }),
   114	            snapback4_core::JobState::Parked => {
   115	                work.parked += 1;
   116	                if shared.personas { shared.refusals.job(&run); }
   117	                snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} parked after {} chunks: {}", run.name, run.chunks, run.error.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));
   118	            }
   119	            _ => {}
   120	        }
   121	        // FIFO engine admission hands waiting requests their turn before the
   122	        // next chunk; yield_now alone cannot make that promise for a mutex.
   123	    }
   124	    if drain || tick % 5 == 0 {
   125	        loop {
   126	            let actions = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| {
   127	                let result = engine.requeue_expired_actions_for(shared.now(), budget);
   128	                if result.as_ref().is_ok_and(|n| *n > 0) { shared.durable_committed(engine); }
   129	                result
   130	            }), drain, "the action lease sweep")?;
   131	            let effects = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| {
   132	                let result = engine.requeue_expired_effects_for(shared.now(), budget);
   133	                if result.as_ref().is_ok_and(|n| *n > 0) { shared.durable_committed(engine); }
   134	                result
   135	            }), drain, "the effect lease sweep")?;
   136	
   137	            if effects > 0 { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: {effects} effect lease{} expired; queued again", if effects == 1 { "" } else { "s" }); }
   138	            if !drain || actions + effects == 0 { break; }
   139	        }
   140	    }
   141	    if drain || tick % 60 == 0 {
   142	        phase(shared, (|| {
   143	            let mut sweep = engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| engine.prepare_asset_sweep_for(shared.now(), budget))?;
   144	            sweep.remove_files()?;
   145	            while !sweep.is_empty() {
   146	                let (_, next) = engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, _| engine.finish_asset_sweep_for(&mut sweep, budget).map(|finished| (finished, sweep)))?;
   147	                sweep = next;
   148	            }
   149	            Ok(())
   150	        })(), drain, "the asset sweep")?;
   151	    }
   152	    Ok(work)
   153	}
   154	
   155	fn delivery(shared: &Shared, drain: bool) -> Result<usize, RuntimeError> {
   156	    let mut chunks = 0;
   157	    while drain || chunks < 500 {
   158	        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { break; }
   159	        if phase(shared, engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| engine.run_timeline_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget)), drain, "timeline delivery")?.is_none() { break; }
   160	        chunks += 1;
   161	    }
   162	    Ok(chunks)
   163	}
   164	
   165	// @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — ordinary-priority bulk work yields
   166	// outside every shared lock. Aged service protects finite backlogs from traffic.
   167	const BULK_AGE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(1);
   168	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
   169	enum BulkKind { Delivery, Recount, Job }
   170	pub(super) struct State {
   171	    next: BulkKind,
   172	    serviced: Instant,
   173	    resume_at: Instant,
   174	    empty_pause: Duration,
   175	    advancing: bool,
   176	    // Explicit mutation-boundary tests pause all background maintenance until drain.
   177	    paused: bool,
   178	    // Separate clock callers (and a dev reset) may wait here; maintenance never takes this lock.
   179	    pub(super) advance: Arc<std::sync::Mutex<()>>,
   180	    #[cfg(test)]
   181	    lose_advance_ack: bool,
   182	}
   183	impl Default for State {
   184	    fn default() -> Self {
   185	        let now = Instant::now();
   186	        Self { next: BulkKind::Delivery, serviced: now, resume_at: now, empty_pause: Duration::ZERO,
   187	            advancing: false, paused: false, advance: Arc::new(std::sync::Mutex::new(())),
   188	            #[cfg(test)] lose_advance_ack: false }
   189	    }
   190	}
   191	impl State {
   192	    pub(super) fn with_pause(paused: bool) -> Self { Self { paused, ..Self::default() } }
   193	    fn aged(&self, now: Instant) -> bool { now.saturating_duration_since(self.serviced) >= BULK_AGE }
   194	    fn eligible(&self, now: Instant, aged_only: bool) -> bool {
   195	        !self.advancing && !self.paused && if aged_only { self.aged(now) } else { now >= self.resume_at }
   196	    }
   197	    fn completed(&mut self, now: Instant, elapsed: Duration, contended: bool, worked: bool) {
   198	        self.next = match self.next { BulkKind::Delivery => BulkKind::Recount, BulkKind::Recount => BulkKind::Job, BulkKind::Job => BulkKind::Delivery };
   199	        self.serviced = now;
   200	        // Target 1/8 CPU duty under contention, charging consumed thread CPU,
   201	        // including atomic overruns. Cap at 100 ms; aged service is independent.
   202	        let cap = Duration::from_millis(100);
   203	        let charged = if contended { (elapsed * 7).min(cap) } else { Duration::ZERO };
   204	        self.empty_pause += charged;
   205	        // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — empty kinds keep their CPU
   206	        // charge, but repay it with the next useful slice or the empty cycle.
   207	        // Avoid two extra scheduler wakeups for every single delivery slice.
   208	        let pause = if worked || self.empty_pause >= cap { self.empty_pause.min(cap) } else { Duration::ZERO };
   209	        self.empty_pause -= pause;
   210	        // An aged slice can finish after bulk flushes empty-cycle debt. Append
   211	        // this charge without discarding repayment still owed in that ordering.
   212	        self.resume_at = self.resume_at.max(now) + pause;
   213	    }
   214	
   215	    fn empty_cycle(&mut self, now: Instant) -> Duration {
   216	        let pause = std::mem::take(&mut self.empty_pause);
   217	        // A timed worker may just have scheduled repayment. Append remaining
   218	        // debt rather than overlapping (and losing) either charge.
   219	        self.resume_at = self.resume_at.max(now) + pause;
   220	        self.resume_at.saturating_duration_since(now)
   221	    }
   222	}
   223	
   224	// Admission owns no maintenance gate. Once admitted, the engine serializes the
   225	// cursor's read -> chunk commit -> advance; the gate only protects small state
   226	// accesses. Ordinary work never waits for a bulk thread to enter the engine.
   227	fn bulk_slice(shared: &Shared, aged_only: bool) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   228	    if !lock(&shared.maintenance).eligible(Instant::now(), aged_only) { return Ok(false); }
   229	    engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| {
   230	        let kind = {
   231	            let state = lock(&shared.maintenance);
   232	            if !state.eligible(Instant::now(), aged_only) { return Ok(false); }
   233	            state.next
   234	        };
   235	        let started = SliceClock::start();
   236	        let result = match kind {
   237	            BulkKind::Delivery => engine.run_timeline_turn_for(shared.now(), budget).map(|r| r.is_some()),
   238	            BulkKind::Recount => engine.run_view_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget).map(|r| r.is_some()),
   239	            BulkKind::Job => {
   240	                let run = engine.run_job_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget);
   241	                for line in engine.take_native_logs() { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: native {line}"); }
   242	                run.map(|run| {
   243	                    if let Some(run) = &run {
   244	                        match run.state {
   245	                            snapback4_core::JobState::Done => snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} done: {} chunks, {} rows written", run.name, run.chunks, run.rows_written),
   246	                            snapback4_core::JobState::Parked => {
   247	                                if shared.personas { shared.refusals.job(run); }
   248	                                snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} parked after {} chunks: {}", run.name, run.chunks, run.error.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));
   249	                            }
   250	                            _ => {}
   251	                        }
   252	                    }
   253	                    run.is_some()
   254	                })
   255	            }
   256	        };
   257	        lock(&shared.maintenance).completed(Instant::now(), started.consumed(), budget == CONTENDED_TIME || shared.engine.has_waiters(), result.as_ref().map_or(true, |worked| *worked));
   258	        result
   259	    })
   260	}
   261	
   262	fn timely_engine_work<T: Default + Send + 'static>(shared: &Shared, run: impl FnOnce(&mut snapback4_core::Engine, Duration, &Shared) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> + Send + 'static) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
   263	    let result = engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| {
   264	        if { let state = lock(&shared.maintenance); state.advancing || state.paused } { return Ok(T::default()); }
   265	        run(engine, budget, shared)
   266	    });
   267	    promote_aged(shared, Instant::now());
   268	    result
   269	}
   270	
   271	fn timely(shared: &Shared, tick: u64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   272	    phase(shared, timely_engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| engine.sweep_expired_for(shared.now(), 2_000, budget)), false, "the expiry sweep")?;
   273	    if !shared.hold_schedules {
   274	        let count = timely_engine_work(shared, move |engine, _, _| Ok(engine.schema().schedules.len()))?;
   275	        for index in 0..count {
   276	            // One atomic boundary per admission, never the entire schedule set.
   277	            // Adoption between slices is harmless: the next tick visits its set.
   278	            let fired = phase(shared, timely_engine_work(shared, move |engine, _, shared| engine.fire_schedule_at(shared.now(), index)), false, "schedules")?;
   279	            for name in fired { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: schedule {name} fired"); }
   280	        }
   281	    }
   282	    if tick % 5 == 0 {
   283	        phase(shared, timely_engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| {
   284	            let result = engine.requeue_expired_actions_for(shared.now(), budget);
   285	            if result.as_ref().is_ok_and(|n| *n > 0) { shared.durable_committed(engine); }
   286	            result
   287	        }), false, "the action lease sweep")?;
   288	        let effects = phase(shared, timely_engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| {
   289	            let result = engine.requeue_expired_effects_for(shared.now(), budget);
   290	            if result.as_ref().is_ok_and(|n| *n > 0) { shared.durable_committed(engine); }
   291	            result
   292	        }), false, "the effect lease sweep")?;
   293	        if effects > 0 { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: {effects} effect leases expired; queued again"); }
   294	    }
   295	    if tick % 60 == 0 {
   296	        phase(shared, (|| {
   297	            let Some(mut sweep) = timely_engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, shared| engine.prepare_asset_sweep_for(shared.now(), budget).map(Some))? else { return Ok(()); };
   298	            // Bounded stale batch; filesystem work owns no engine lock.
   299	            sweep.remove_files()?;
   300	            while !sweep.is_empty() {
   301	                // A clock drain that began between file removal and retirement
   302	                // owns the sweep now. Its durable metadata also permits retry
   303	                // on the next asset tick; do not spin on skipped slices.
   304	                let Some((finished, next)) = timely_engine_work(shared, move |engine, budget, _| engine.finish_asset_sweep_for(&mut sweep, budget).map(|finished| Some((finished, sweep))))? else { break; };
   305	                sweep = next;
   306	                if finished == 0 { break; }
   307	            }
   308	            Ok(())
   309	        })(), false, "the asset sweep")?;
   310	    }
   311	    Ok(())
   312	}
   313	
   314	pub(super) fn maintain(shared: Arc<Shared>) {
   315	    let mut tick = 0;
   316	    let mut last_tick = Instant::now();
   317	    loop {
   318	        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
   319	        {
   320	            let state = lock(&shared.maintenance);
   321	            if !state.advancing && !state.paused && let Err(error) = shared.ephemeral.sweep(shared.now()) { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: ephemeral sweep: {error}"); }
   322	        }
   323	        if last_tick.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(1) {
   324	            tick += 1; last_tick = Instant::now();
   325	            // One bounded reclamation slice; abandoned bytes remain charged
   326	            // until their actual records are dropped. No engine lock is held.
   327	            lock(&shared.following).reclaim(shared.now(), snapback4_core::Ceilings { heap_bytes: 1 << 20, ..Default::default() });
   328	            if let Err(error) = timely(&shared, tick) { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: maintenance failed: {error}"); }
   329	        }
   330	        promote_aged(&shared, Instant::now());
   331	        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
   332	    }
   333	}
   334	
   335	fn promote_aged(shared: &Shared, now: Instant) {
   336	    if lock(&shared.maintenance).eligible(now, true) {
   337	        if let Err(error) = bulk_slice(shared, true) { super::adoption::background_failure(shared, "aged bulk maintenance", &error); }
   338	    }
   339	}

     3	
     4	/** Authenticated development and staging return this exact bearer's current live lifetime.
     5	 * Matching the dispatch bearer is the caller's responsibility. Same-bearer
     6	 * preservation is monotonic, so a delayed old reply cannot undo a jump. */
     7	export function reconciledTiming(session: Session, response: Response): Session {
     8	  if (response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-timing") !== "staging") return session;
     9	  const expiry = Number(response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-expires"));
    10	  const capText = response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-cap");
    11	  const cap = capText === null ? undefined : Number(capText);
    12	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(expiry) || expiry <= 0) return session;
    13	  const read = isReadToken(session.token);
    14	  if (read && (cap === undefined || !Number.isSafeInteger(cap) || cap < expiry)) return session;
    15	  if (!read && cap !== undefined) return session;
    16	  const expiresAt = Math.max(session.expiresAt, expiry);
    17	  const capExpiresAt = cap === undefined ? undefined : Math.max(session.capExpiresAt ?? 0, cap);
    18	  if (expiresAt === session.expiresAt && capExpiresAt === session.capExpiresAt) return session;
    19	  return { ...session, expiresAt, ...(capExpiresAt === undefined ? {} : { capExpiresAt }) };
    20	}
    21	
    22	export function requestMatchesSession(request: { url: string; authorization: string | null }, base: string, session: Session): boolean {
    23	  return (request.url === base || request.url.startsWith(base + "/")) && request.authorization === `Bearer ${session.token}`;
    24	}
    25	
    26	/** Expired local metadata may be superseded by a preserving development or staging jump.
    27	 * Read-only probing creates no session and never widens or refreshes one. */
    28	export async function restoreStagingTiming(base: string, session: Session, fetcher: typeof fetch): Promise<Session | null> {
    29	  const aborter = new AbortController();
    30	  const timer = setTimeout(() => aborter.abort(), 10_000);
    31	  (timer as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
    32	  try {
    33	    const response = await fetcher(`${base}/probe?scope=1`, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${session.token}` }, signal: aborter.signal });
    34	    if (!response.ok) return null;
    35	    const next = reconciledTiming(session, response);
    36	    const stamp = Number(response.headers.get("x-snapback-time"));
    37	    if (next === session || !Number.isSafeInteger(stamp) || next.expiresAt <= stamp) return null;
    38	    // Consume the bounded ordinary probe body before blessing restoration.
    39	    const { readResponse } = await import("./http-response.ts");
    40	    const body = await readResponse(response);
    41	    return body.ok === true && body.viewer === session.principal && body.scope === (isReadToken(session.token) ? "read" : "full") ? next : null;
    42	  } catch { return null; }
    43	  finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
    44	}

   341	pub(super) fn bulk(shared: Arc<Shared>) {
   342	    loop {
   343	        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
   344	        let observed = *lock(&shared.seq);
   345	        let mut did_work = false;
   346	        for _ in 0..3 {
   347	            if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
   348	            match bulk_slice(&shared, false) {
   349	                Ok(worked) => did_work |= worked,
   350	                Err(error) => {
   351	                    super::adoption::background_failure(&shared, "bulk maintenance admission", &error);
   352	                    // Refusal is not an idle slice: preserve the due scheduler
   353	                    // and retry on the next bounded turn, with a logged reason.
   354	                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
   355	                    did_work = true;
   356	                    break;
   357	                }
   358	            }
   359	            let pause = lock(&shared.maintenance).resume_at.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
   360	            if !pause.is_zero() {
   361	                // No shared lock survives this pause. Request commits do not
   362	                // cancel repayment; empty kinds do not idle an active cycle.
   363	                std::thread::sleep(pause.min(Duration::from_millis(100)));
   364	            }
   365	        }
   366	        if !did_work {
   367	            loop {
   368	                let pause = lock(&shared.maintenance).empty_cycle(Instant::now());
   369	                if pause.is_zero() { break; }
   370	                std::thread::sleep(pause.min(Duration::from_millis(100)));
   371	            }
   372	            let seq = lock(&shared.seq);
   373	            if *seq == observed {
   374	                drop(shared.committed.wait_timeout(seq, Duration::from_millis(100)).unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()));
   375	            }
   376	        }
   377	    }

   380	// Reset even when a debug/unwind request panics mid-drain. Engine poison
   381	// recovery and durable work cursors retain the normal retry semantics.
   382	struct Advancing<'a>(&'a Shared);
   383	impl Drop for Advancing<'_> {
   384	    fn drop(&mut self) {
   385	        let mut state = lock(&self.0.maintenance);
   386	        state.advancing = false;
   387	        state.next = BulkKind::Delivery;
   388	    }
   389	}

   391	fn completed_drain(work: Work, stopping: bool) -> Result<Work, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
   392	    if stopping {
   393	        return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED", "clock", "clock advanced, but host shutdown interrupted the drain; inspect queued work instead of repeating the advance").guide("clock"));
   394	    }
   395	    Ok(work)
   396	}

   398	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — staging grants clock control, never fixture or persona authority.
   399	pub(super) fn staging(shared: &Shared) -> Result<bool, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
   400	    shared.engine.call(|engine, _| engine.store().deployment_environment().ok().flatten().as_deref() == Some("staging"))
   401	}
   402	fn clock_service(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<Option<String>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
   403	    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — real-user dev clock authority is independent of fixture doors.
   404	    if shared.personas {
   405	        match super::session_identity::principal_of(request,shared) {
   406	            Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind == "service" && principal == "service:operator" => return Ok(Some(principal)),
   407	            _ if shared.dev_doors() && crate::auth::bearer_token(request).is_err() => return Ok(None),
   408	            _ => return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "development clock control requires a full service:operator token").guide("clock")),
   409	        }
   410	    }
   411	    if !staging(shared)? { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
   412	    match super::session_identity::principal_of(request,shared) {
   413	        Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind == "service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Ok(Some(principal)),
   414	        _ => Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "staging clock control requires a full service token").guide("clock")),
   415	    }
   416	}
   417	fn checkpoint_clock(engine: &mut snapback4_core::Engine, delta: i64, before: i64, preserve: bool, checkpoint: &snapback4_core::metadata::StagingClock) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   418	    let result = engine.checkpoint_staging_clock_at(delta, before, preserve, checkpoint);
   419	    #[cfg(feature = "postgres")]
   420	    if matches!(result, Err(RuntimeError::CommitOutcomeUnknown(_))) { crate::lifecycle::uncertain_clock_commit(); }

   428	fn advance_error(error: &snapback4_core::Refusal, published: Option<i64>, milliseconds: i64) -> (u16, serde_json::Value) {
   429	    match published {
   430	        Some(now) => (500, json!({"now":now,"advancedMs":milliseconds,"reportIncomplete":true,"denied":incomplete_report(error)})),

   435	pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
   436	    let service = match clock_service(&request, shared) {
   437	        Ok(service) => service,
   438	        Err(why) => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})),
   439	    };

   441	        Ok(payload) => payload,
   442	        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
   443	    };
   444	    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
   445	        None => false,
   446	        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
   447	        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
   448	    };
   449	    let boolean = |key: &str, default: bool| -> Result<bool, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
   450	        match payload.get(key) {
   451	            None => Ok(default), Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => Ok(*value),
   452	            Some(_) => Err(crate::dev_clock::input(format!("{key} must be a boolean"))),
   453	        }
   454	    };
   455	    let (drain, allow_held) = match boolean("drain", true).and_then(|drain| Ok((drain, boolean("allowHeld", false)?))) {
   456	        Ok(options) => options,
   457	        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   458	    };

   462	        Some("preserve") if service.is_some() => true,
   463	        Some("expire") => false,

   468	        .and_then(|duration| if duration == "0ms" && drain { Ok(0) } else { crate::dev_clock::duration(duration) }) {
   469	        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,

   471	    };

   476	    let _advance = loop {
   477	        if Instant::now() >= deadline {
   478	            let why = super::committer::queue_error(snapback4_core::engine::command::SubmitError::Full).refusal();
   479	            return respond(request, 503, json!({"denied":why}));
   480	        }

   482	            Ok(guard) => break guard,
   483	            Err(std::sync::TryLockError::Poisoned(error)) => break error.into_inner(),
   484	            Err(std::sync::TryLockError::WouldBlock) => std::thread::park_timeout(Duration::from_millis(1)),
   485	        }

   491	    let facts = super::RequestFacts::capture(&request);

   502	        let floor = shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire);
   503	        let effective_before = clock.now().max(floor);
   504	        let mut next = clock.clone();

   506	        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — refuse a saved boundary before any clock/session effect.
   507	        if drain && !allow_held && !shared.hold_schedules {
   508	            crate::dev_clock_store::refuse_held(engine, &before, clock.now(), now)?;

   511	        if !shared.personas {

   513	            // A raw jump below that floor advances no business time and cannot
   514	            // extend credentials or leases. Selection must never see an older
   515	            // raw process time and revive an already expired family.

   528	        *clock = next;
   529	        *lock(&publication) = Some(now);
   530	        let offset = clock.offset();
   531	        let skew = now - snapback4_core::now_ms();

   534	        } else { String::new() };
   535	        let lifetime = if shared.personas { "development offset lasts until restart" } else { "offset persists with this staging store across restarts" };
   536	        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (now includes the authoritative floor); {lifetime}{isolated}");
   537	        Ok((now, clock.now(), offset, skew, message, before, boundaries))

   540	    #[cfg(test)]

   542	        Err(super::committer::queue_error(snapback4_core::engine::command::SubmitError::OutcomeUnknown).refusal())
   543	    } else { advanced };
   544	    let (now, raw_now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
   545	        Ok(result) => result,
   546	        Err(error) => { let (status, reply) = advance_error(&error, *lock(&published), milliseconds); return respond(request, status, reply); }
   547	    };
   548	    #[cfg(test)] super::test_work(&shared.clock_advanced);
   549	    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
   550	    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
   551	    let drained = (|| {
   552	        if !drain { return Ok(Work::default()); }
   553	        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
   554	        if each_boundary {
   555	            for (index, at) in boundaries {
   556	                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, move |engine, _, shared| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(shared.now(), index, at))?);
   557	            }
   558	        }
   559	        let mut work = work_at(shared, true, 0, Some(now))?;
   560	        work.schedules += crossed.len();
   561	        work.fired.extend(crossed);
   562	        // Clearing the durable pause is the last part of a completed drain.
   563	        // Failure leaves both the record and runtime paused; never replay time.
   564	        if !shared.personas && !crate::lifecycle::stopping() {
   565	            shared.engine.attempt(|engine, shared| {
   566	                let clock = lock(&shared.clock);
   567	                let checkpoint = snapback4_core::metadata::StagingClock { now: clock.now(), offset: clock.offset(), paused: false };
   568	                checkpoint_clock(engine, 0, checkpoint.now, false, &checkpoint)
   569	            })?;
   570	        }
   571	        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
   572	    })();
   573	    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
   574	        Ok(work) => {
   575	            lock(&shared.maintenance).paused = !drain;
   576	            let fired = work.fired.clone();
   577	            let schedules = shared.engine.call(move |engine, shared| crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(engine, &before, &fired, now, raw_now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port())));
   578	            let schedules = match schedules {
   579	                Ok(schedules) => schedules,
   580	                Err(why) => return respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now,"advancedMs":milliseconds,"reportIncomplete":true,"denied":incomplete_report(&why)})),
   581	            };
   582	            let (fired, held) = crate::dev_clock_store::outcomes(&schedules);
   583	            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "sessionNotice":if preserve_sessions { crate::dev_clock::PRESERVED_SESSION_NOTICE } else { crate::dev_clock::SESSION_NOTICE }, "maintenancePaused":!drain, "sessionsPreserved":preserve_sessions, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules, "fired":fired, "held":held}))
   584	        }
   585	        // Time remains advanced on failure. Stay paused until an explicit drain;
   586	        // never retry an ambiguous advance to resume pending work.
   587	        Err(error) => {
   588	            let schedules = shared.engine.call(move |engine, shared| {
   589	
   590	            let after = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(engine);
   591	            let fired: Vec<_> = engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().filter(|(i, _)| {
   592	                matches!((before.get(*i).copied().flatten(), after.get(*i).copied().flatten()), (Some(old), Some(new)) if new > old)
   593	            }).map(|(_, schedule)| schedule.name.clone()).collect();
   594	            let mut schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(engine, &before, &fired, now, raw_now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
   595	            for (index, schedule) in schedules.iter_mut().enumerate() {
   596	                if schedule["fired"] != true && !shared.hold_schedules && before.get(index).copied().flatten().is_some_and(|old| old < engine.schema().schedules[index].boundary(now)) {
   597	                    schedule["holdCode"] = "maintenance-failed".into();
   598	                    schedule["reason"] = "clock advanced but maintenance stopped before this due boundary was queued; inspect clock and queued jobs before continuing; do not replay the advance".into();
   599	                }
   600	            }
   601	                schedules
   602	            });
   603	            let schedules = match schedules {
   604	                Ok(schedules) => schedules,
   605	                Err(why) => return respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now,"advancedMs":milliseconds,"reportIncomplete":true,"denied":incomplete_report(&why)})),
   606	            };
   607	            let (fired, held) = crate::dev_clock_store::outcomes(&schedules);
   608	            respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "sessionNotice":if preserve_sessions { crate::dev_clock::PRESERVED_SESSION_NOTICE } else { crate::dev_clock::SESSION_NOTICE }, "maintenancePaused":true, "sessionsPreserved":preserve_sessions, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "schedules":schedules, "fired":fired, "held":held, "reportIncomplete":true, "denied":incomplete_report(&error)}))
   609	        },
   610	    }
   611	}

     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — exact-source refresh repairs derived artifacts without changing source or fixture evidence.

     5	
     6	pub(super) struct SavedVersion { path: PathBuf, files: Vec<(PathBuf, Vec<u8>)>, complete: bool, refresh: bool }
     7	impl Drop for SavedVersion {
     8	    fn drop(&mut self) { if !self.complete && !self.refresh { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.path); } }
     9	}
    10	impl SavedVersion {
    11	    pub(super) fn prepare(project: &Project, sources: &[(String,String)], bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound, options: &Options, deployment: &std::path::Path) -> Result<Option<Self>,String> {
    12	        let Some(name) = options.flags.get("save-version") else {return Ok(None);};
    13	        if sources.is_empty() || sources.iter().all(|(_,text)|text.trim().is_empty()) {return Err("not saved yet: a saved version needs nonempty Quarry source".into());}
    14	        if options.flags.contains_key("check") {return Err("--save-version captures a successful deployment; omit --check to save, or omit --save-version for a read-only preview".into());}
    15	        if project.root.canonicalize().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.starts_with(deployment.canonicalize().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?) {
    16	            return Err("cannot save a version inside deployment custody; run deploy from the application's source directory".into());
    17	        }
    18	        let suffix = name.strip_prefix("versions/v").ok_or("--save-version needs a new project-relative versions/vN directory")?;
    19	        if suffix.is_empty() || suffix.starts_with('0') || !suffix.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_digit()) {return Err("--save-version needs versions/vN with a positive numeric N".into());}
    20	        let parent=project.root.join("versions");
    21	        if std::fs::symlink_metadata(&parent).is_ok_and(|m|m.file_type().is_symlink()) {return Err("--save-version refuses a symlinked versions directory".into());}
    22	        std::fs::create_dir_all(&parent).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    23	        let path=project.root.join(name);
    24	        let refresh = match std::fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
    25	            Ok(meta) if meta.file_type().is_symlink() || !meta.is_dir() => return Err(format!("cannot refresh {name}: saved version must be a real directory")),
    26	            Ok(_) => {
    27	                let selected=sources.iter().cloned().collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    28	                let existing=crate::client_compatibility::sources(&path).map_err(|e|format!("cannot refresh {name}: {e}"))?.into_iter().collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    29	                if existing != selected {return Err(format!("cannot refresh {name}: selected source differs from the saved source; choose a new versions/vN"));}
    30	                true
    31	            }
    32	            Err(error) if error.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {std::fs::create_dir(&path).map_err(|e|format!("cannot save {name}: {e}"))?;false}
    33	            Err(error) => return Err(format!("cannot inspect {name}: {error}")),
    34	        };
    35	        let mut files = if refresh {Vec::new()} else {sources.iter().map(|(name,text)|(PathBuf::from(name),text.as_bytes().to_vec())).collect()};
    36	        files.push((PathBuf::from("generated/api.ts"),snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(bound).into_bytes()));
    37	        files.push((PathBuf::from("generated/package.json"),crate::project::GENERATED_PACKAGE.as_bytes().to_vec()));
    38	        let mut saved=Self {path,files,complete:false,refresh};
    39	        let fixture = match options.flags.get("schema").map(PathBuf::from) {
    40	            None=>project.root.join("fixtures"),
    41	            Some(source) if source.is_dir()=>source.join("fixtures"),
    42	            Some(source) if source.file_name().is_some_and(|n|n=="schema.q")=>source.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures"),
    43	            Some(source)=>source.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures",source.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy())),
    44	        };
    45	        if fixture.is_dir() && !refresh {
    46	            let mut entries=std::fs::read_dir(&fixture).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    47	            entries.sort_by_key(|e|e.file_name());
    48	            for entry in entries {
    49	                if entry.path().extension().is_some_and(|e|e=="jsonl") || entry.file_name()=="test.sweep.json" {
    50	                    if !entry.file_type().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_file() {return Err("saved fixtures must be regular files".into());}
    51	                    saved.files.push((PathBuf::from("fixtures").join(entry.file_name()),std::fs::read(entry.path()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?));
    52	                }
    53	            }
    54	        }
    55	        if !refresh {saved.files.push((PathBuf::from("fixtures/.gitkeep"),Vec::new()));}
    56	        Ok(Some(saved))
    57	    }
    58	    pub(super) fn finish(&mut self, release: u64, source_hash: &str) -> Result<(),String> {
    59	        self.files.push((PathBuf::from("release.json"),format!("{}\n",serde_json::json!({"release":release,"sourceHash":source_hash,"cliVersion":env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")})).into_bytes()));
    60	        for (name,bytes) in &self.files {
    61	            let path=self.path.join(name);
    62	            std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    63	            if self.refresh {
    64	                let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("refresh-{}",std::process::id()));
    65	                std::fs::write(&temporary,bytes).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    66	                std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    67	            } else {
    68	                use std::io::Write;
    69	                let mut file=std::fs::OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true).open(path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    70	                file.write_all(bytes).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    71	            }
    72	        }
    73	        self.complete=true;
    74	        eprintln!("{} release {release} sources, generated client, contemporary fixtures and release.json at {}",if self.refresh {"refreshed"} else {"saved"},self.path.display());
    75	        Ok(())
    76	    }
    77	}

   356	
   357	export interface ExhaustPagesOptions {
   358	  /** Refuse rather than loop forever when a backend returns bad continuations. */
   359	  maxPages?: number;
   360	  /** Names the read in a refusal without exposing its rows or cursor. */
   361	  label?: string;
   362	}
   363	type ProofPage<Row> =
   364	  | { readonly data: readonly Row[]; readonly next?: Cursor | null; readonly complete: boolean; readonly cut: ReadCut; readonly existence?: "present" | "absent" | "partial" }
   365	  | { readonly denied: Refusal }
   366	  | { readonly loading: true };
   367	/** Read every page by passing each opaque `next` back unchanged. The callback
   368	 * receives null first. Refusals, loading, malformed/repeated cursors and a
   369	 * capped or incomplete page fail instead of returning a partial snapshot. */
   370	/** @throws E_PROOF_PAGE for a refused, loading, malformed, repeated, incomplete or over-limit traversal; callback errors propagate. */
   371	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — proof traversal must never promote capped or partial reads to complete evidence.
   372	export async function exhaustPages<Row>(readPage: (cursor: Cursor | null) => Promise<ProofPage<Row>>, options: ExhaustPagesOptions = {}): Promise<Row[]> {
   373	  const maxPages = options.maxPages ?? 1_000, label = options.label ?? "paged read";
   374	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(maxPages) || maxPages < 1) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: maxPages must be a positive safe integer`);
   375	  const rows: Row[] = [], seen = new Set<string>();
   376	  let cursor: Cursor | null = null;
   377	  for (let pageNumber = 1; pageNumber <= maxPages; pageNumber++) {
   378	    const page = await readPage(cursor);
   379	    if (!page || typeof page !== "object") throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} returned no read envelope`);
   380	    if ("denied" in page) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} refused ${page.denied.code}: ${page.denied.message}`);
   381	    if ("loading" in page) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} is still loading`);
   382	    if (!Array.isArray(page.data)) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} data is not an array`);
   383	    if (page.cut !== null) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} is incomplete (${page.cut === undefined ? "missing" : page.cut} cut)`);
   384	    if (page.complete !== true) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} is incomplete (complete must be true)`);
   385	    if (page.existence === "partial") throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} has partial existence`);
   386	    rows.push(...page.data);
   387	    const next = page.next ?? null;
   388	    if (next === null) return rows;
   389	    if (typeof next !== "string" || next.length === 0) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} returned a malformed cursor`);
   390	    if (seen.has(next)) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} returned a repeated cursor`);
   391	    seen.add(next);
   392	    cursor = next as Cursor;
   393	  }
   394	  throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: exceeded maxPages ${maxPages}`);
   395	}

   401	/** @throws observation deadline with scoped locator, last text and visible headings; selector ambiguity is propagated. */
   402	export async function waitForFresh(locator: Locator, previous: string | null, options: number | { within?: Locator; timeoutMs?: number } = 30_000): Promise<string> {
   403	  const timeoutMs = deadline(typeof options === "number" ? options : options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
   404	  let substring = substringLocators.get(locator);
   405	  locator = registeredLocator(locator);
   406	  if (typeof options !== "number" && options.within) {
   407	    locator = registeredLocator(options.within.locator(locator));
   408	    if (substring) substring = { locator: options.within.locator(substring.locator), scoped: true };
   409	  }

   104	    // @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — an older decoder refuses ordered insert rules rather than misjudging them.
   105	    ("insert-rule-order", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| ordered(&t.rules.insert))),
   106	    // @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — a decoder that orders only int/time in insert rules refuses a money or decimal order by name.
   107	    ("insert-rule-order-units", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| ordered_units(t, &t.rules.insert))),
   108	    ("row-keyed-view", |s| s.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.row_key.is_some())),
   109	    // @ref LLP 3000.007.003#30-aggregate-target-identity-and-migration — older devices compute canonical-JSON
   110	    // aggregate ids and would silently miss digest-addressed target rows.
   111	    ("maintained-id-sha256-v1", |s| s.maintains.iter().any(|m| m.row_key.is_none())),
   112	    // @ref LLP 3000.039#8 — an older engine or device must not delete with the old (orphaning) semantics.
   113	    ("reference-actions", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| !t.on_delete.is_empty())),
   114	    // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.4 — pre-Wave-12 devices neither retire expiry
   115	    // through actions nor queue an unproved same-id physical predecessor.
   116	    ("expiry-reference-actions-v1", |s| s.tables.iter().any(|(name, table)| table.is_expiring() && s.is_referenced(name))),
   117	    ("read-through", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| t.through.is_some()) || s.maintains.iter().any(|m| m.parent_key.is_some() || !m.aliases.is_empty())),
   118	    // Device projections keep column metadata but omit private maintains.
   119	    ("maintained-column", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| !t.maintained.is_empty()) || s.maintains.iter().any(|m| m.private.is_some())),
   120	    ("recorded-attribution", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| !t.recorded.is_empty())),
   121	    ("sequence-by", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| !t.sequences.is_empty())),
   122	    // @ref LLP 3000.040#4-fences-and-compatibility — an older decoder names the bounded int it cannot check.
   123	    ("int-range", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| t.columns.values().any(int_range))),
   124	    // @ref LLP 3000.040#4-fences-and-compatibility — civil keys and calendar schedules.
   125	    ("calendar-month", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| t.indexes.values().any(|i| i.components.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, IndexComponent::Calendar(key) if key.civil()))))
   126	        || s.maintains.iter().any(|m| m.computed.values().any(|key| key.civil()))),
   127	    ("calendar-schedule", |s| s.schedules.iter().any(|schedule| schedule.months > 0)),
   128	    ("timeline", |s| s.timelines.iter().any(|t| t.version == 1)),
   129	    ("timeline-v2", |s| s.timelines.iter().any(|t| t.version == 2)),
   130	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#3 — an older device would page a merged plan as an eager merge and refuse its server cursors; it refuses the backend by name.
   131	    ("branch-merge-v1", |s| s.operations.values().any(|o| o.branch_merge)),
   132	    ("branch-membership-v1", |s| s.operations.values().any(|o| o.branch_membership)),
   133	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — v2 fences metered uncapped discovery and row-id point lanes from v1 devices.
   134	    ("branch-pages-v2", |s| s.operations.values().any(|o| o.branch_pages_v2)),
   135	    ("branch-pages-v3", |s| s.operations.values().any(|o| o.branch_pages_v3)),
   136	    ("branch-pages-v5", |s| s.operations.values().any(|o| o.branch_pages_v5)),
   137	    ("branch-pages-v4", |s| s.operations.values().any(|o| o.branch_pages_v4)),
   138	    ("branch-membership-v2", |s| s.operations.values().any(|o| o.branch_membership_v2)),
   139	    // Source slots alter negative coverage, prediction and application row projection.
   140	    ("timeline-event-slots", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| t.columns.keys().any(|field| timeline::is_slot(field)))),
   141	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.000.000#2.3 — a decoder that ignored `also` would hold only the first arm and lose rows.
   142	    ("union-audience", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| t.sync.as_ref().is_some_and(Sync::is_union))),
   143	    ("time-audiences-v1", |s| s.tables.values().any(|t| t.not_before.is_some())),
   144	];
   145	
   146	fn int_range(kind: &ColumnType) -> bool {
   147	    match kind {
   148	        ColumnType::IntRange { .. } => true,
   149	        ColumnType::Optional(inner) | ColumnType::List { of: inner, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of: inner, .. } => int_range(inner),
   150	        _ => false,
   151	    }
   152	}
   153	
   154	fn ordered_units(table: &Table, rule: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
   155	    let units = |term: &RuleTerm| matches!(term, RuleTerm::Field(f) if matches!(table.columns.get(f), Some(ColumnType::Decimal { .. } | ColumnType::Money { .. })));
   156	    match rule {
   157	        RuleExpr::Compare(_, l, r) => units(l) || units(r),
   158	        RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().any(|item| ordered_units(table, item)),
   159	        RuleExpr::Not(inner) => ordered_units(table, inner),
   160	        _ => false,
   161	    }
   162	}
   163	
   164	fn ordered(rule: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
   165	    match rule {
   166	        RuleExpr::Compare(..) => true,
   167	        RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().any(ordered),
   168	        RuleExpr::Not(inner) => ordered(inner),
   169	        _ => false,
   170	    }
   171	}
   172	
   173	impl Schema {
   174	    pub fn supported_capabilities() -> Vec<String> { CAPABILITIES.iter().map(|(name, _)| (*name).into()).collect() }
   175	    fn has_receipt_columns(&self) -> bool {
   176	        self.tables.iter().any(|(name, table)| table.maintained.keys().any(|field|
   177	            self.receipt_dependencies.get(name).is_some_and(|fields| fields.contains(field))))
   178	    }
   179	    pub fn required_capabilities(&self) -> BTreeSet<String> {
   180	        CAPABILITIES.iter().filter(|(_, needs)| needs(self)).map(|(name, _)| (*name).into()).collect()
   181	    }
   182	}
   183	
   184	impl Serialize for Schema {
   185	    fn serialize<S: Serializer>(&self, serializer: S) -> Result<S::Ok, S::Error> {
   186	        let required = self.required_capabilities();
   187	        // The derive's field order, omissions and bytes are unchanged for
   188	        // ordinary schemas, including the serialized conformance corpus.
   189	        if required.is_empty() { return Wire::serialize(self, serializer); }
   190	        let mut wire = self.clone();
   191	        for table in wire.tables.values_mut() {
   192	            table.constraints.retain(|c| !matches!(c, Constraint::Recorded));
   193	        }
   194	        let mut carrier = wire.tables.first_entry().ok_or_else(||
   195	            serde::ser::Error::custom("E_SCHEMA_CAPABILITY: required capabilities need a table carrier"))?;
   196	        carrier.get_mut().constraints.push(Constraint::Capabilities(required));
   197	        Wire::serialize(&wire, serializer)
   198	    }
   199	}
   200	
   201	impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for Schema {
   202	    fn deserialize<D: Deserializer<'de>>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Self, D::Error> {
   203	        // The private remote derive uses the ordinary
   204	        // streaming serde codec. No JSON-value or content-buffer replay.
   205	        let mut schema = Wire::deserialize(deserializer)?;
   206	        let mut declared = BTreeSet::new();
   207	        let mut carriers = 0;
   208	        let mut legacy = false;
   209	        for table in schema.tables.values_mut() {
   210	            let mut recorded = 0;
   211	            table.constraints.retain(|constraint| {
   212	                if let Constraint::Capabilities(names) = constraint {
   213	                    carriers += 1;
   214	                    declared.extend(names.iter().cloned());
   215	                    false
   216	                } else if matches!(constraint, Constraint::Recorded) {
   217	                    recorded += 1;
   218	                    false
   219	                } else { true }
   220	            });
