   336	            }
   337	            drop(receipts);drop(q);
   338	            // An entering inactive receipt restores exact native cells. Those
   339	            // preimages outrank a different leaving declaration's substitution.
   340	            for entering in next.downfills.iter().filter(|f|f.table==fill.table) {
   341	                if let Some(e)=evidence(tx,t,id,&entering.down_key())? {if !e.active {forward_outputs.extend(e.reversed.into_keys());}}
   342	            }
   343	            let mut pruned_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
   344	            let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT octet_length(d.definition),d.definition FROM _backfill_pruned p JOIN _backfill_definitions d ON d.digest=p.definition WHERE p.t=?1 AND p.id=?2 ORDER BY p.definition")?;
   345	            let mut receipts=q.query(params![t,id])?;
   346	            while let Some(r)=receipts.next()? {
   347	                let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap((r.get::<_,i64>(0)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   348	                pruned_outputs.extend(decode::<Backfill>(&r.get::<_,String>(1)?)?.outputs);
   349	            }
   350	            drop(receipts);drop(q);
   351	            if target_outputs.iter().any(|c|pruned_outputs.contains(c)) {bad.add(id,"forward predecessor preimage was explicitly pruned");return Ok(())}
   352	            let mut projected_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
   353	            for c in &target_outputs {
   354	                if forward_outputs.contains(c){continue}
   355	                // An active receipt means this cell was supplied by this down
   356	                // declaration on the preceding rollback.  Its archived copy
   357	                // is therefore a prior down result, not a predecessor
   358	                // preimage, and the next cycle must issue a fresh receipt so
   359	                // edits under the older release remain conflict checked.
   360	                if prior.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.active && e.reversed.contains_key(c)) {
   361	                    projected_outputs.insert(c.clone());
   362	                    continue;
   363	                }
   364	                // A non-null retained predecessor value is an exact preimage.
   365	                // Legacy rollback loops could manufacture optional null cells;
   366	                // the explicit down declaration is the author's substitution
   367	                // policy for that otherwise ambiguous evidence-free case.
   368	                let retained=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND data!=?4)",params![t,id,c,canonical_json(&Value::Null)],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?;
   369	                // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — an identical-type rename presents the current business value in the old spelling.
   370	                if !retained || fill.name.starts_with("$rename:down:") {projected_outputs.insert(c.clone());}
   371	            }
   372	            if projected_outputs.len()<target_outputs.len(){preimages+=1;if preimage_ids.len()<5{preimage_ids.push(id.to_owned());}}
   373	            if projected_outputs.is_empty(){return Ok(())}
   374	            if prior.is_none() && tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition=?3)",params![t,id,key],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {
   375	                bad.add(id,"reverse projection evidence was explicitly pruned");return Ok(())
   376	            }
   377	            let native_source=row_source(tx,t,id,sources.get(&fill.table))?;
   378	            if let Some(source)=native_source.as_deref() {if let Some(c)=projected_outputs.iter().find(|c|source.columns.get(*c).is_some_and(protected)||source_owned(source,c)) {
   379	                bad.add(id,&format!("native preimage {}.{c} requires reference, asset or lifetime custody; keep a typed bridge",fill.table));return Ok(())
   380	            }}
   381	            let mut args=BTreeMap::new();
   382	            for (c,kind) in &fill.inputs {
   383	                let value=source_value(tx,t,id,&row,c,native_source.as_deref().or(Some(&previous.tables[&fill.table])),&mut Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort))?;
   384	                if crate::engine::validate::validate_column(&fill.table,c,kind,value.as_ref()).is_err(){bad.add(id,&format!("current reverse input {}.{c} is absent or invalid",fill.table));return Ok(())}
   385	                args.insert(c.clone(),value.unwrap_or(Value::Null));
   386	            }
   387	            let inputs=Value::Row(args.clone().into());let mut context=migration_context(tx,now)?;context.args=args;
   388	            for (c,kind) in &fill.inputs {
   389	                let kind=native_source.as_deref().and_then(|s|s.columns.get(c)).filter(|k|protected(k)).unwrap_or(kind);
   390	                let value=inputs.field(c)?;let unchanged=prior.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|e.inputs.field(c).ok()==Some(value));
   391	                capture_input_refs(tx,t,id,c,&input_ref(&key,c),kind,value,unchanged)?;
   392	                if input_lifetime_lost(tx,t,id,&key,c)? {bad.add(id,&format!("reverse input {c} lost its parent lifetime; use a typed bridge"));return Ok(())}
   393	            }
   394	            let mut reads=BTreeSet::new();collect_reads(&fill.program.body,&mut reads);
   395	            let needed=reads.difference(&rebuilt_reads).cloned().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   396	            if !needed.is_empty(){rebuild_reads(tx,&source,&needed)?;tx.clear_activation_scan();tx.set_meta("activation_population","true")?;rebuilt_reads.extend(needed);}
   397	            let mut runner=Runner::new(tx,&source,context,Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort),0);
   398	            let patch=match runner.run(&fill.program){Ok(Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f))=>f.into_iter().collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(),Ok(_)=>{bad.add(id,"down projection did not return an object");return Ok(())},Err(e)=>{bad.add(id,&e.to_string());return Ok(())}};
   399	            if runner.read_cut().is_some(){bad.add(id,"reverse lookup did not complete; reduce its bounded work");return Ok(())}
   400	            if patch.keys().cloned().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()!=fill.outputs{return Err(refusal("down projection returned undeclared output fields"))}
   401	            let patch:BTreeMap<_,_>=patch.into_iter().filter(|(c,_)|projected_outputs.contains(c)).collect();
   402	            let reversed=projected_outputs.iter().map(|c|{
   403	                // Each rollback owns this cycle's current physical preimage,
   404	                // including newer writes and absence since the last restoration.
   405	                let value=fields.get(c).cloned();
   406	                (c.clone(),value)
   407	            }).collect();
   408	            let e=Evidence{computed:BTreeMap::new(),authored:BTreeSet::new(),reversed,retained:BTreeSet::new(),absent:BTreeSet::new(),epoch:tx.meta("generation")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<i64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0).saturating_add(1),active:false,inputs,before:BTreeMap::new(),after:patch.clone()};
   409	            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO temp._downfill_pending VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",params![t,id,key,canonical_json(&patch),canonical_json(&e)])?;count+=1;Ok(())
   410	        })?;
   411	        if bad.count>0{return Err(refusal(format!("down {} {}: {}",fill.table,fill.name,bad.describe())))}
   412	        if preimages>0 {report.backfills.push(format!("{} {} retained predecessor preimages on {preimages} rows; sample ids [{}]; these rows read stale under the older release until forward; newer source cells remain retained for forward",fill.table,fill.name,preimage_ids.join(", ")));}
   413	        if count>0{report.backfills.push(format!("{} {} projected {count} {} down",fill.table,fill.name,if count==1{"row"}else{"rows"}));}
   414	    }
   415	    Ok(true)
   416	}
   417	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#191-declared-old-views-and-renames — all down expressions observe the assembled newer snapshot before any forward preimage is restored.
   418	fn apply_staged_downfills(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   419	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition,patch,evidence,octet_length(patch)+octet_length(evidence) FROM temp._downfill_pending ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;let mut rows=stmt.query([])?;
   420	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;
   421	        let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap((r.get::<_,i64>(5)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192)).map_err(|err|refusal(format!("down projection staged reversal evidence exceeds its read budget: {err}; reduce the per-row migration image")))?;
   422	        let patch:BTreeMap<String,Value>=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(3)?)?;let e:Evidence=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(4)?)?;
   423	        let (mut row,in_r)=load_image(tx,t,&id)?.ok_or_else(||refusal("down source lifetime ended before its staged patch"))?;if let Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=&mut row{f.extend(patch)}
   424	        patch_retained(tx,t,&id,e.after.iter().map(|(c,v)|(c.as_str(),Some(v))))?;
   425	        if in_r {tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;}save(tx,t,&id,&key,&e,false)?;
   426	    }
   427	    drop(rows);drop(stmt);tx.conn().execute_batch("DROP TABLE temp._downfill_pending;")?;tx.set_meta("activation_population","true")?;Ok(())
   428	}
   429	
   430	pub(super) fn transition(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,previous:Option<&Schema>,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,now:i64,report:&mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   431	    if next.backfills.is_empty() && next.downfills.is_empty()
   432	        && previous.is_none_or(|s|s.backfills.is_empty() && s.downfills.is_empty()) && !present(tx)? { return Ok(()); }
   433	    enable(tx)?;
   434	    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TEMP TABLE _backfill_unprovided(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
   435	    let _source_mask=SourceMask(tx);
   436	    for fill in &next.backfills {
   437	        fill.admit(next).map_err(refusal)?;
   438	        if fill.name.starts_with("$rename:up:") {
   439	                if fill.inputs.len()!=1 || fill.outputs.len()!=1 {return Err(refusal("generated column rename must have exactly one input and output"))}
   440	
   441	            let source_kind=fill.inputs.keys().next().and_then(|c|sources.get(&fill.table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(c)));
   442	            let target_kind=fill.outputs.iter().next().and_then(|c|next.tables[&fill.table].columns.get(c));
   443	            if source_kind.is_some() && source_kind!=target_kind {return Err(refusal(format!("column rename on {} must use identical old and new cell types",fill.table)))}
   444	        }
   445	        if let Some(old)=sources.get(&fill.table) {for c in &fill.outputs {
   446	            if source_owned(old,c) {
   447	                return Err(refusal(format!("backfill {} {}: output {c} is a historical lifetime or engine field; preserve its source meaning in a bridge",fill.table,fill.name)));
   448	            }
   449	        }}
   450	    }
   451	    let mut down_names=BTreeSet::new();let mut down_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
   452	    for fill in &next.downfills {
   453	        fill.admit_down(next).map_err(refusal)?;
   454	        if !down_names.insert((&fill.table,&fill.name)) || fill.outputs.iter().any(|c|!down_outputs.insert((&fill.table,c))) {
   455	            return Err(refusal(format!("down projections require distinct names and disjoint outputs on {} {}",fill.table,fill.name)))
   456	        }
   457	    }
   458	    // A name is immutable within a continuous lineage. Replacing a transform
   459	    // deliberately goes through an old-source/bridge transition first.
   460	    if let Some(old)=previous {for f in &next.backfills {if let Some(p)=old.backfills.iter().find(|p|p.table==f.table && p.name==f.name && p.key()!=f.key()) {return Err(refusal(format!("backfill {} {} changed historical meaning; old digest {}; new digest {}; use a new name in a bridge release rather than replacing historical meaning",f.table,f.name,p.key(),f.key())));}}}

    15	) -> Value {

    25	        )

    15	pub fn sql(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    16	    match inspect(options) {
    17	        Ok(()) => 0,
    18	        Err((code, message)) => {
    19	            if options.json { println!("{}", json!({ "denied": { "code": code, "message": message } })); }
    20	            else { eprintln!("{code}: {message}"); }
    21	            1

    26	fn inspect(options: &Options) -> Result<()> {
    27	    if crate::store_config::StoreConfig::resolve(options).map_err(|e| ("E_STORE_CONFIG", e))?.is_some() { return Err(("E_STORE_UNSUPPORTED", "inspection is outside the admitted store profile".into())); }
    28	    let [statement] = options.positionals.as_slice() else { return Err(("E_INPUT", format!("usage: {}; {CATALOG_HINT}", crate::cli_flags::synopsis("sql")))); };
    29	    if let Some(flag) = misplaced_flag(statement) {
    30	        return Err(("E_INPUT", format!("{flag} is inside the SQL argument; put CLI options after its closing quote: snapback4 sql 'SELECT …' {flag}{}", if flag == "--release" { " <dir>" } else if flag == "--limit" { " N" } else { "" })));
    31	    }
    32	    let command = catalog::command(statement)?;
    33	    let body = if command.is_none() { select_body(statement)? } else { String::new() };
    34	    let limit = match options.flags.get("limit") {
    35	        None => 200,
    36	        Some(value) => value.parse::<u32>().ok().filter(|n| *n > 0).ok_or(("E_INPUT", "--limit must be a positive 32-bit integer".into()))?,
    37	    };
    38	    let dormant = options.flags.contains_key("dormant");
    39	    let all_releases = options.flags.contains_key("all-releases");
    40	    if dormant && all_releases { return Err(("E_INPUT", "sql takes either --dormant or --all-releases, not both".into())); }
    41	    if (dormant || all_releases) && !options.flags.contains_key("release") {
    42	        return Err(("E_INPUT", "sql --dormant and --all-releases require an explicit --release <dir> deployment".into()));
    43	    }
    44	    let path = match options.flags.get("release") {
    45	        Some(dir) => Path::new(dir).join("store.sqlite"),
    46	        None => project_or_exit().store_path(),
    47	    };
    48	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#8 — developer SQL is read-only, never rule-filtered
    49	    let conn = AnyInspection::open(&path).map_err(|error| {
    50	        let (code, message) = error;
    51	        (code, format!("{message}{}", store_hint(&path, options)))
    52	    })?;
    53	    conn.begin_snapshot()?;
    54	    // Catalog, compiled columns and rows all belong to the same WAL snapshot.
    55	    let (generation, active_schema) = catalog::active_schema(&conn)?;
    56	    let schema = if dormant || all_releases { conn.retained_schema(active_schema)? } else { active_schema };
    57	    let retained = conn.dormant_inventory()?;
    58	    let mode = if dormant { "dormant retained records" } else if all_releases { "active and retained records" } else { "active records" };
    59	    let header = format!("sql: developer's own store {} — {mode}; read-only, not rule-filtered", path.display());
    60	    if let Some(command) = command {
    61	        let result = catalog::inspect(&conn, &schema, command)?;
    62	        if options.json {
    63	            println!("{}", json!({ "header": header, "generation": generation, "tables": result, "ruleFiltered": false }));
    64	        } else {
    65	            eprintln!("{header}");
    66	            catalog::print(&result, generation);
    67	        }
    68	        if !dormant && !all_releases { dormant_notice(dormant_rows(&retained), "rerun SQL with --dormant and an explicit --release to inspect them"); }
    69	        return Ok(());
    70	    }
    71	    conn.build_retained_views(&schema, dormant, all_releases, |name| eprintln!("sql: skipping {name}: ephemeral rows live only in the serving process's memory"))?;
    72	    let object_rows = options.flags.contains_key("json-objects");
    73	    let snapback4_core::store_port::InspectionRows { columns, rows, more } = conn.query(&body, limit, object_rows,
    74	        |message| query_error(&schema, &retained, message),
    75	        |columns| snapback4_core::inspection::check_output_names(columns, object_rows),
    76	        |columns| objects::kinds(&body, &schema, columns, dormant || all_releases).map_err(|e| ("E_INPUT", e)))?;
    77	    if options.json {
    78	        let rows = if object_rows {
    79	            Value::Array(rows.into_iter().map(|values| Value::Object(columns.iter().cloned().zip(values).collect())).collect())
    80	        } else { json!(rows) };
    81	        println!("{}", json!({ "header": header, "columns": columns, "rows": rows, "limit": limit, "more": more, "ruleFiltered": false, "inspection": mode }));
    82	    } else {
    83	        eprintln!("{header}");
    84	        let cells: Vec<Vec<String>> = rows.iter().map(|row| row.iter().map(|v| match v { Value::String(s) => s.escape_debug().to_string(), _ => v.to_string() }).collect()).collect();
    85	        let widths: Vec<usize> = columns.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, name)| cells.iter().map(|row| row[i].chars().count()).chain([name.chars().count()]).max().unwrap()).collect();
    86	        let line = |cells: &[String]| cells.iter().zip(&widths).map(|(cell, width)| format!("{cell}{}", " ".repeat(width.saturating_sub(cell.chars().count())))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" | ");
    87	        println!("{}", line(&columns));
    88	        println!("{}", widths.iter().map(|n| "-".repeat(*n)).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("-+-"));
    89	        for row in &cells { println!("{}", line(row)); }
    90	        eprintln!("{} row(s){}", rows.len(), if more { " — more rows exist; increase --limit or refine the SELECT" } else { "" });
    91	    }
    92	    if options.json && more { eprintln!("sql: result truncated at {limit} rows; increase --limit or refine the SELECT"); }
    93	    if !dormant && !all_releases { dormant_notice(dormant_rows(&retained), "rerun SQL with --dormant and an explicit --release to inspect them"); }
    94	    Ok(())
    95	}

    97	pub(super) fn dormant_rows(items: &[snapback4_core::release_history::DormantItem]) -> u64 {
    98	    items.iter().filter(|item| matches!(item.kind.as_str(), "table" | "rows")).map(|item| item.rows).sum()
    99	}
   100	
   101	pub(super) fn dormant_notice(rows: u64, advice: &str) {
   102	    if rows > 0 {
   103	        eprintln!("{rows} dormant {} retained; {advice}", if rows == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" });
   104	    }
   105	}
   106	
   107	pub(super) fn retained_table_rows(items: &[snapback4_core::release_history::DormantItem], table: &str) -> u64 {
   108	    items.iter().filter(|item| matches!(item.kind.as_str(), "table" | "rows") && item.path.eq_ignore_ascii_case(table)).map(|item| item.rows).sum()
   109	}
   110	
   111	fn query_error(schema: &Schema, retained: &[snapback4_core::release_history::DormantItem], message: String) -> (&'static str, String) {
   112	    let missing = message.strip_prefix("no such table: ").map(str::trim);
   113	    if let Some(table) = missing {
   114	        let rows = retained_table_rows(retained, table.trim_matches(['`', '"', '\'']));
   115	        if rows > 0 {
   116	            return ("E_INPUT", format!("SQL: {message}; {rows} retained dormant {} exist for {table}; inspect them with `snapback4 sql 'SELECT * FROM {table}' --release <dir> --dormant`", if rows == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" }));
   117	        }
   118	    }
   119	    snapback4_core::inspection::query_error(schema, message)
   120	}

   123	
   124	/// Population advice only for a newly declared/changed window, before adoption.
   125	// @ref LLP 3000.007#8 — bounded catalog probes never adopt or acquire a writer lock.
   126	pub(super) fn window_populations(path: &Path, candidate: &Schema) -> Result<Vec<(String, u32, u64)>> {
   127	    if !candidate.visible_tables().any(|(_, table)| table.ephemeral.is_none() && table.sync.as_ref().is_some_and(|s| s.horizon.is_some())) { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
   128	    let conn = AnyInspection::open(path)?;
   129	    conn.begin_snapshot()?;
   130	    let adopted = conn.inspection_adopted()?;
   131	    if !adopted { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
   132	    let (_, active) = catalog::active_schema(&conn)?;
   133	    let mut populations = Vec::new();
   134	    for (name, table) in candidate.visible_tables() {
   135	        let Some(sync) = &table.sync else { continue; };
   136	        let Some(horizon) = &sync.horizon else { continue; };
   137	        let Some(previous) = active.tables.get(name) else { continue; };
   138	        if previous.sync == table.sync || table.ephemeral.is_some() { continue; }
   139	        let threshold = u64::from(horizon.last) * 10;
   140	        if threshold == 0 || threshold > catalog::COUNT_LIMIT { continue; }
   141	        let count = catalog::row_count(&conn, name, threshold)?;
   142	        if count == threshold { populations.push((name.clone(), horizon.last, count)); }
   143	    }
   144	    Ok(populations)
   145	}
   146	
   147	/// Strip SQL comments without disturbing quotes, and permit a single trailing
   148	/// terminator. SQLite still parses and authorizes the actual statement.
   149	fn select_body(sql: &str) -> Result<String> {
   150	    if sql.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(PRIVATE) { return Err(("E_SQL_INTERNAL", format!("private inspection views cannot be named in SQL; {CATALOG_HINT}"))); }
   151	    let mut chars = sql.chars().peekable();
   152	    let mut body = String::new();
   153	    let mut ended = false;
   154	    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
   155	        if c == '-' && chars.peek() == Some(&'-') {
   156	            for c in chars.by_ref() { if c == '\n' { break; } }
   157	            body.push('\n');
   158	        } else if c == '/' && chars.peek() == Some(&'*') {
   159	            chars.next();
   160	            while let Some(c) = chars.next() { if c == '*' && chars.peek() == Some(&'/') { chars.next(); break; } }
   161	            body.push(' ');
   162	        } else if c.is_whitespace() { body.push(c); }
   163	        else if ended { return Err(("E_SQL_READ_ONLY", "sql accepts exactly one SELECT statement".into())); }
   164	        else if c == ';' { ended = true; }
   165	        else if matches!(c, '\'' | '"' | '`' | '[') {
   166	            let end = if c == '[' { ']' } else { c };
   167	            body.push(c);
   168	            while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
   169	                body.push(c);
   170	                if c == end {
   171	                    if end != ']' && chars.peek() == Some(&end) { body.push(chars.next().unwrap()); }
   172	                    else { break; }
   173	                }
   174	            }
   175	        } else { body.push(c); }
   176	    }
   177	    let first = body.trim_start().split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_alphabetic()).next().unwrap_or_default();
   178	    if !first.eq_ignore_ascii_case("select") && !first.eq_ignore_ascii_case("with") {
   179	        return Err(("E_SQL_READ_ONLY", "sql accepts one SELECT or WITH … SELECT statement".into()));
   180	    }
   181	    Ok(body)
   182	}
   183	
   184	// A quoted SQL string may itself contain a CLI-looking value. Only flag tokens
   185	// outside SQLite quotes are a misplaced option (including SQLite line comments).
   186	fn misplaced_flag(statement: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
   187	    let mut chars = statement.char_indices().peekable();
   188	    while let Some((offset, c)) = chars.next() {
   189	        if matches!(c, '\'' | '"' | '`' | '[') {
   190	            let end = if c == '[' { ']' } else { c };
   191	            while let Some((_, c)) = chars.next() {
   192	                if c == end {
   193	                    if end != ']' && chars.peek().is_some_and(|(_, c)| *c == end) { chars.next(); }
   194	                    else { break; }
   195	                }
   196	            }
   197	        } else if c == '/' && chars.peek().is_some_and(|(_, c)| *c == '*') {
   198	            chars.next();
   199	            while let Some((_, c)) = chars.next() {
   200	                if c == '*' && chars.peek().is_some_and(|(_, c)| *c == '/') { chars.next(); break; }
   201	            }
   202	        } else if c == '-' {
   203	            for flag in ["--release", "--dormant", "--all-releases", "--limit", "--json-objects", "--json"] {
   204	                if let Some(tail) = statement[offset..].strip_prefix(flag) {
   205	                    if tail.is_empty() || tail.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) || tail.starts_with('=') { return Some(flag); }
   206	                }
   207	            }
   208	        }
   209	    }
   210	    None
   211	}
   212	
   213	/// Missing development stores never imply that a neighbouring release is the
   214	/// store to inspect. Name the explicit selection without opening or adopting it.
   215	pub(super) fn store_hint(path: &Path, options: &Options) -> String {
   216	    if options.flags.contains_key("release") || path.exists() { return String::new(); }
   217	    let Some(root) = path.parent().and_then(Path::parent) else { return String::new(); };
   218	    let mut dirs = Vec::new();
   219	    if root.join("store.sqlite").is_file() { dirs.push(root.to_path_buf()); }
   220	    if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(root) {
   221	        for entry in entries.flatten() {
   222	            if entry.file_name() != ".snapback4" && entry.file_type().is_ok_and(|kind| kind.is_dir())
   223	                && entry.path().join("store.sqlite").is_file() { dirs.push(entry.path()); }
   224	        }
   225	    }
   226	    dirs.sort();
   227	    if dirs.is_empty() { return String::new(); }
   228	    format!("; no development store at {}. Deployment stores are present at {}; select one explicitly with --release <dir>",
   229	        path.display(), dirs.iter().map(|dir| dir.display().to_string()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))
   230	}

     8	// Only structural table identifiers change. Literal values, column names,
     9	// operation names and authored defaults are never table identifiers.
    10	fn identifiers(value:&mut serde_json::Value,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>) {
    11	    match value {
    12	        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs {identifiers(x,map)},
    13	        serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>for (key,x) in fields {
    14	            if key=="Literal" || key=="defaults" {continue;}
    15	            if matches!(key.as_str(),"table"|"parent") {if let Some(name)=x.as_str().and_then(|s|map.get(s)){*x=serde_json::Value::String(name.clone());continue;}}
    16	            identifiers(x,map);
    17	        },_=>()
    18	    }
    19	}
    20	fn mapped(name:&str,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->String {map.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())}
    21	fn remap<T:serde::Serialize+serde::de::DeserializeOwned>(value:&T,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<T,RuntimeError> {
    22	    let mut json=serde_json::to_value(value).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;identifiers(&mut json,map);
    23	    serde_json::from_value(json).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))
    24	}
    25	pub(crate) fn schema(mut schema:Schema,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<Schema,RuntimeError> {
    26	    if map.is_empty(){return Ok(schema)}
    27	    schema.tables=std::mem::take(&mut schema.tables).into_iter().map(|(name,table)|Ok((mapped(&name,map),remap(&table,map)?))).collect::<Result<_,RuntimeError>>()?;
    28	    for m in &mut schema.maintains {*m=remap(m,map)?;m.source=mapped(&m.source,map);m.target=mapped(&m.target,map);}
    29	    for f in &mut schema.backfills {fill(f,map,false)?;}
    30	    for f in &mut schema.downfills {fill(f,map,true)?;}
    31	    for e in &mut schema.extractions {e.child=mapped(&e.child,map);}
    32	    // Remaining table-bearing structural metadata is normalized through the
    33	    // same typed codec; maintains' source/target are handled explicitly above.
    34	    // These maps contain column identifiers, including arbitrary keys such as
    35	    // "parent". Normalize their typed table slots, never their column strings.
    36	    let mut extractions=std::mem::take(&mut schema.extractions);
    37	    for e in &mut extractions {e.parent=mapped(&e.parent,map);}
    38	    let columns=std::mem::take(&mut schema.asset_renames).into_iter().map(|(name,columns)|(mapped(&name,map),columns)).collect();
    39	    schema.table_renames.clear();schema=remap(&schema,map)?;
    40	    schema.extractions=extractions;schema.asset_renames=columns;
    41	    Ok(schema)
    42	}
    43	fn fill(f:&mut crate::schema::backfill::Backfill,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>,down:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    44	    *f=remap(f,map)?;
    45	    f.program.name=format!("{}{}{}",if down{"down"}else{"backfill"},f.table,f.name);Ok(())
    46	}
    47	pub(super) fn prepare(conn:&Connection,next:&Schema,catalog:&mut BTreeMap<String,i64>)->Result<BTreeMap<String,String>,RuntimeError> {
    48	    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_table_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    49	    if !exists && next.table_renames.is_empty(){return Ok(BTreeMap::new())}
    50	    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _table_renames(name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,origin TEXT NOT NULL) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    51	    let mut names:BTreeMap<String,String>=conn.prepare("SELECT name,origin FROM _table_renames")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    52	    for (new,old) in &next.table_renames {
    53	        if new==old || !next.tables.contains_key(new) || next.tables.contains_key(old){return Err(refuse("table rename requires a distinct predecessor-only source"))}
    54	        let origin=names.get(old).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||old.clone());
    55	        if names.get(new).is_some_and(|root|root!=&origin){return Err(refuse(format!("table rename {old} → {new} conflicts with an independent retained identity")))}

    58	    }
    59	    let mut selected=BTreeMap::<String,String>::new();
    60	    for name in next.tables.keys() {if let Some(root)=names.get(name) {if let Some(other)=selected.insert(root.clone(),name.clone()){return Err(refuse(format!("table aliases {other} and {name} cannot be active together")))}}}
    61	    let map:BTreeMap<String,String>=names.iter().filter_map(|(name,root)|selected.get(root).filter(|new|*new!=name).map(|new|(name.clone(),new.clone()))).collect();
    62	    for (old,new) in &map {

    67	        conn.execute("UPDATE _assets SET home_table=?1 WHERE home_table=?2",params![new,old])?;

    70	        }
    71	    }
    72	    // Retained typed definitions must use the current public table names too.
    73	    for table in ["_dormant_tables","_dormant_columns","_dormant_narrow_rows"] {

    81	}
    82	fn history(conn:&Connection,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    83	    for (table,down) in [("_backfill_definitions",false),("_downfill_definitions",true)] {
    84	        if !conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[table],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {continue;}
    85	        let rows:Vec<(String,String)>=conn.prepare(&format!("SELECT digest,definition FROM {table}"))?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    86	        for (old,text) in rows {let mut f:crate::schema::backfill::Backfill=serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;fill(&mut f,map,down)?;let new=if down {f.down_key()}else{f.key()};if old==new{continue;}
    87	            conn.execute(&format!("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO {table} VALUES(?1,?2)"),params![new,canonical_json(&f)])?;
    88	            for ledger in ["_backfill_evidence","_backfill_pruned","_backfill_absence"] {conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {ledger} SET definition=?1 WHERE definition=?2"),params![new,old])?;}
    89	            conn.execute("UPDATE _backfill_absence SET definition=?1 WHERE definition=?2",params![format!("$native:{new}"),format!("$native:{old}")])?;
    90	            conn.execute("UPDATE _backfill_absence SET definition=?1 WHERE definition=?2",params![format!("$authored:{new}"),format!("$authored:{old}")])?;
    91	            for c in f.inputs.keys() {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_refs SET column_name=?1 WHERE column_name=?2",params![format!("$backfill:{new}:{c}"),format!("$backfill:{old}:{c}")])?;}
    92	            conn.execute(&format!("DELETE FROM {table} WHERE digest=?1"),[old])?;
    93	        }
    94	    }Ok(())
    95	}

   745	                            // @ref LLP 3000.014.000#71-dormant-restoration-identities-are-a-relation — live policy restamps current inputs after preserving parent lifetime.
   746	                            if through.live.is_none() && !crate::rows::Rows::restored_live_authority(tx,name,id)? && through.fields.iter().any(|(capture, field)| {
   747	                                row.field(capture)
   748	                                    .ok()
   749	                                    .is_some_and(|value| Some(value) != parent.field(field).ok())
   750	                            }) {
   751	                                bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} parent {parent_id}: captured audience/identity changed",name,through.reference));
   752	                            }
   753	                        }
   754	                    }
   755	                    Ok(())
   756	                })?;
   757	                if bad.count > 0 {
   758	                    return Err(refuse(format!(
   759	                        "table {name}: {}; restore cannot rebind historical audience/deadline captures; restore the original parent reference and audience under the current release, then `snapback4 rollback <dir> --to N`, or explicitly `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`",
   760	                        bad.describe()
   761	                    )));
   762	                }
   763	            }
   764	        }
   765	    }
   766	    Ok(())
   767	}

     9	fn principal(value: &Value) -> Option<&str> {
    10	    match value {
    11	        Value::Id(value) => value
    12	            .strip_prefix("service:")
    13	            .filter(|name| !name.is_empty())
    14	            .map(|_| value.as_str()),
    15	        _ => None,
    16	    }
    17	}
    18	
    19	fn rule(rule: &RuleExpr, seats: &mut BTreeSet<String>, positive: bool) {
    20	    match rule {
    21	        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Literal(value))
    22	        | RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(value), RuleTerm::Viewer)
    23	            if positive =>
    24	        {
    25	            if let Some(value) = principal(value) {
    26	                seats.insert(value.into());
    27	            }
    28	        }
    29	        RuleExpr::And(rules) | RuleExpr::Or(rules) => rules
    30	            .iter()
    31	            .for_each(|rule| self::rule(rule, seats, positive)),
    32	        RuleExpr::Not(rule) => self::rule(rule, seats, !positive),
    33	        _ => {}
    34	    }
    35	}
    36	
    37	fn expression(expr: &Expr, seats: &mut BTreeSet<String>, positive: bool) {
    38	    match expr {
    39	        Expr::Binary {
    40	            op: BinaryOp::Eq | BinaryOp::SameValueZero,
    41	            left,
    42	            right,
    43	        } => match (&**left, &**right, positive) {
    44	            (Expr::Input(Input::Viewer), Expr::Literal(value), true)
    45	            | (Expr::Literal(value), Expr::Input(Input::Viewer), true) => {
    46	                if let Some(value) = principal(value) {
    47	                    seats.insert(value.into());
    48	                }
    49	            }
    50	            _ => {}
    51	        },
    52	        Expr::Binary {
    53	            op: BinaryOp::And | BinaryOp::Or,
    54	            left,
    55	            right,
    56	        } => {
    57	            expression(left, seats, positive);
    58	            expression(right, seats, positive);
    59	        }
    60	        Expr::Unary {
    61	            op: snapback4_core::ir::UnaryOp::Not,
    62	            value,
    63	        } => expression(value, seats, !positive),
    64	        Expr::Block { body, value } => {
    65	            statements(body, seats);
    66	            expression(value, seats, positive);
    67	        }
    68	        _ => {}
    69	    }
    70	}
    71	
    72	fn statements(body: &[Stmt], seats: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {
    73	    for statement in body {
    74	        match statement {
    75	            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition, seats, true),
    76	            Stmt::If {
    77	                then_body,
    78	                else_body,
    79	                ..
    80	            } => {
    81	                statements(then_body, seats);
    82	                statements(else_body, seats);
    83	            }
    84	            Stmt::For { body, .. } => statements(body, seats),
    85	            Stmt::Check { condition, .. } => expression(condition, seats, true),
    86	            Stmt::WriteTarget {
    87	                condition: Some(condition),
    88	                ..
    89	            } => statements(&condition.setup, seats),
    90	            _ => {}
    91	        }
    92	    }
    93	}
    94	
    95	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — minted seats stay ordinary principals; this is advisory schema evidence, never a grant.
    96	pub(crate) fn collect(bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound) -> BTreeSet<String> {
    97	    let mut seats = BTreeSet::new();
    98	    for table in bound.schema.tables.values() {
    99	        for rule in [
   100	            &table.rules.read,
   101	            &table.rules.insert,
   102	            &table.rules.update,
   103	            &table.rules.delete,
   104	        ] {
   105	            self::rule(rule, &mut seats, true);
   106	        }
   107	        for rule in table.column_reads.values() {
   108	            self::rule(rule, &mut seats, true);
   109	        }
   110	    }
   111	    for program in &bound.programs {
   112	        statements(&program.program.body, &mut seats);
   113	    }
   114	    seats.extend(
   115	        bound
   116	            .schema
   117	            .schedules
   118	            .iter()
   119	            .filter_map(|schedule| schedule.service.clone()),
   120	    );
   121	    seats.extend(
   122	        bound
   123	            .schema
   124	            .routes
   125	            .values()
   126	            .filter_map(|route| route.service.clone()),
   127	    );
   128	    seats
   129	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	//! SQLite erases booleans to integers. Recover direct column types from the
     2	//! adopted schema and SQL projection, never by guessing from names or 0/1.
     3	// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.26 — positional JSON stays compatible; boolean recovery is presentation on the operator's adopted snapshot.
     4	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — direct schema-proven media cells use their ordinary wire JSON in object rows.
     5	use snapback4_core::{inspection::InspectionColumnKind as Kind, schema::{ColumnType, Schema}};
     6	use sqlparser::{ast::{Expr, ObjectName, Query, SelectItem, SelectItemQualifiedWildcardKind, SetExpr, Statement, TableFactor}, dialect::SQLiteDialect, parser::Parser};
     7	use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
     8	
     9	type Columns = Vec<(String, Kind)>;
    10	type Tables = BTreeMap<String, Columns>;
    11	
    12	pub(super) fn kinds(sql: &str, schema: &Schema, count: usize, retained: bool) -> Result<Vec<Kind>, String> {
    13	    let mut tables: Tables = schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, table)| {
    14	        let mut columns = vec![("id".into(), Kind::Plain)];
    15	        columns.extend(table.columns.iter().filter(|(name, _)| *name != "id")
    16	            .map(|(name, kind)| (name.to_ascii_lowercase(), if matches!(kind.required(), ColumnType::Bool) { Kind::Bool } else if kind.contains_asset() { Kind::Media } else { Kind::Plain })));
    17	        if retained {columns.extend([("_snapback_state".into(),Kind::Plain),("_snapback_source".into(),Kind::Plain),("_snapback_columns".into(),Kind::Plain)]);}
    18	        (name.to_ascii_lowercase(), columns)
    19	    }).collect();
    20	    let statements = Parser::parse_sql(&SQLiteDialect {}, sql).map_err(|e| format!("--json-objects SQL projection: {e}; use --json for SQLite-specific syntax"))?;
    21	    let [Statement::Query(query)] = statements.as_slice() else { return Err("--json-objects takes a SELECT".into()); };
    22	    let columns = query_columns(query, &mut tables)?;
    23	    if columns.len() != count { return Err("--json-objects cannot determine this projection's column types; select explicit columns or use --json".into()); }
    24	    Ok(columns.into_iter().map(|(_, kind)| kind).collect())
    25	}
    26	
    27	fn name(name: &ObjectName) -> Option<String> {
    28	    // SQLite schema qualification bypasses CTE shadowing; do not erase it.
    29	    if name.0.len() != 1 { return None; }
    30	    name.0.last()?.as_ident().map(|id| id.value.to_ascii_lowercase())
    31	}
    32	
    33	fn query_columns(query: &Query, inherited: &mut Tables) -> Result<Columns, String> {
    34	    let mut tables = inherited.clone();
    35	    if let Some(with) = &query.with {
    36	        if with.recursive { return Err("--json-objects does not infer recursive CTE types; use --json".into()); }
    37	        for cte in &with.cte_tables {
    38	            if tables.contains_key(&cte.alias.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase()) {
    39	                return Err("--json-objects does not infer shadowing CTEs; use --json".into());
    40	            }
    41	            let mut columns = query_columns(&cte.query, &mut tables)?;
    42	            for (column, alias) in columns.iter_mut().zip(&cte.alias.columns) { column.0 = alias.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase(); }
    43	            tables.insert(cte.alias.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase(), columns);
    44	        }
    45	    }
    46	    set_columns(&query.body, &mut tables)
    47	}
    48	
    49	fn relation(factor: &TableFactor, tables: &mut Tables) -> Result<(String, Columns), String> {
    50	    match factor {
    51	        TableFactor::Table { name: object, alias, args: None, .. } => {
    52	            let key = name(object).ok_or("--json-objects does not infer schema-qualified relations; use --json")?;
    53	            let columns = tables.get(&key).ok_or("--json-objects needs application tables or typed CTEs")?.clone();
    54	            Ok((alias.as_ref().map(|a| a.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase()).unwrap_or(key), columns))
    55	        }
    56	        TableFactor::Derived { subquery, alias, .. } => Ok((alias.as_ref().map(|a| a.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase()).unwrap_or_default(), query_columns(subquery, tables)?)),
    57	        _ => Err("--json-objects cannot infer this table expression; use --json".into()),
    58	    }
    59	}
    60	
    61	fn set_columns(body: &SetExpr, tables: &mut Tables) -> Result<Columns, String> {
    62	    match body {
    63	        SetExpr::Query(q) => query_columns(q, tables),
    64	        SetExpr::SetOperation { left, right, .. } => {
    65	            let mut left = set_columns(left, tables)?;
    66	            let right = set_columns(right, tables)?;
    67	            if left.len() != right.len() { return Err("incompatible SQL projections".into()); }
    68	            for (l, r) in left.iter_mut().zip(right) { if l.1 != r.1 { l.1 = Kind::Plain; } }
    69	            Ok(left)
    70	        }
    71	        SetExpr::Select(select) => {
    72	            let mut sources = Vec::new();
    73	            for from in &select.from {
    74	                sources.push(relation(&from.relation, tables)?);
    75	                for join in &from.joins { sources.push(relation(&join.relation, tables)?); }
    76	            }
    77	            let mut output = Vec::new();
    78	            for item in &select.projection {
    79	                match item {
    80	                    SelectItem::Wildcard(_) => output.extend(sources.iter().flat_map(|(_, cols)| cols.clone())),
    81	                    SelectItem::QualifiedWildcard(SelectItemQualifiedWildcardKind::ObjectName(object), _) => {
    82	                        let key = name(object).ok_or("unsupported wildcard")?;
    83	                        let (_, cols) = sources.iter().find(|(alias, _)| *alias == key).ok_or("unknown wildcard table")?;
    84	                        output.extend(cols.clone());
    85	                    }
    86	                    SelectItem::UnnamedExpr(expr) => {
    87	                        let mut direct = expr;
    88	                        while let Expr::Nested(inner) = direct { direct = inner; }
    89	                        let label = match direct {
    90	                            Expr::Identifier(id) => id.value.clone(),
    91	                            Expr::CompoundIdentifier(ids) => ids.last().unwrap().value.clone(),
    92	                            _ => expr.to_string(),
    93	                        };
    94	                        output.push((label.to_ascii_lowercase(), kind(expr, &sources)));
    95	                    }
    96	                    SelectItem::ExprWithAlias { expr, alias } => output.push((alias.value.to_ascii_lowercase(), kind(expr, &sources))),
    97	                    _ => return Err("unsupported object-row projection; use --json".into()),
    98	                }
    99	            }
   100	            let mut names = BTreeSet::new();
   101	            if output.iter().any(|(name, _)| !names.insert(name)) {
   102	                return Err("--json-objects needs unique names in every projection; alias duplicate columns".into());
   103	            }
   104	            Ok(output)
   105	        }
   106	        _ => Err("--json-objects needs a SELECT projection; use --json".into()),
   107	    }
   108	}
   109	
   110	fn kind(expr: &Expr, sources: &[(String, Columns)]) -> Kind {
   111	    let (table, column) = match expr {
   112	        Expr::Nested(expr) => return kind(expr, sources),
   113	        Expr::Identifier(id) => (None, &id.value),
   114	        Expr::CompoundIdentifier(ids) if ids.len() == 2 => (Some(ids[0].value.to_ascii_lowercase()), &ids[1].value),
   115	        _ => return Kind::Plain, // Computed SQL expressions retain SQLite's own types.
   116	    };
   117	    let found: Vec<Kind> = sources.iter().filter(|(alias, _)| table.as_ref().is_none_or(|t| t == alias))
   118	        .flat_map(|(_, cols)| cols.iter().filter(|(name, _)| name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(column)).map(|(_, kind)| *kind)).collect();
   119	    // An unqualified duplicate only reaches execution when SQLite resolved it
   120	    // through USING/NATURAL. All contributors must agree before typing it bool.
   121	    found.first().copied().filter(|first| found.iter().all(|kind| kind == first)).unwrap_or(Kind::Plain)
   122	}

   791	pub fn stop_release(options: &Options) -> i32 {
   792	    let result = (|| {
   793	        if crate::store_config::StoreConfig::resolve(options)?.is_some() {
   794	            return Err("E_STORE_UNSUPPORTED: stop is outside the admitted store profile".into());
   795	        }
   796	        let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
   797	        match crate::release_control::request(&dir, crate::release_control::Command::Stop) {
   798	            Ok(_) => {
   799	                crate::store_owner::wait_released(&dir.join("store.sqlite"), std::time::Duration::from_secs(10))?;
   800	                println!("store stopped: {}", dir.display());
   801	                Ok(())
   802	            }
   803	            Err(control) => match crate::store_owner::wait_released(&dir.join("store.sqlite"), std::time::Duration::ZERO) {
   804	                Ok(()) => {
   805	                    println!("store already stopped: {}", dir.display());
   806	                    Ok(())
   807	                }
   808	                Err(_) => Err(control),
   809	            },
   810	        }
   811	    })();
   812	    match result {
   813	        Ok(()) => 0,
   814	        Err(error) => { eprintln!("stop: {error}"); 1 }
   815	    }
   816	}

   818	pub(crate) fn release_bind(options: &Options) -> Result<std::net::IpAddr, String> {
   819	    let bind = options
   820	        .flags
   821	        .get("bind")
   822	        .map(String::as_str)
   823	        .unwrap_or("127.0.0.1");
   824	    let address: std::net::IpAddr = bind
   825	        .parse()
   826	        .map_err(|_| "--bind must be a literal IPv4 or IPv6 address".to_owned())?;
   827	    if !address.is_loopback() {
   828	        if !options.flags.contains_key("i-understand-cleartext") {
   829	            return Err("non-loopback HTTP sends passwords and bearer tokens in cleartext; use a loopback listener behind a TLS proxy, or explicitly pass --i-understand-cleartext".into());

   833	        );
   834	    }
   835	    Ok(address)
   836	}

    11	pub(crate) fn typescript(project:&Project,bound:&Bound)->io::Result<String>{
    12	    let(text,warnings)=report(project,bound)?;
    13	    for warning in warnings {
    14	        if snapback4_lang::client_contracts::is_note(&warning) {eprintln!("NOTE {warning}");}

    17	    Ok(text)
    18	}
    19	pub(crate) fn report(project: &Project, bound: &Bound) -> io::Result<(String,Vec<String>)> {
    20	    let path = project.root.join("snapback/client-compatibility.json");
    21	    let text = match std::fs::read_to_string(path) {
    22	        Ok(text) => text,
    23	        Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok((snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(bound),Vec::new())),
    24	        Err(error) => return Err(error),
    25	    };
    26	    let bad = |message: String| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, format!("client-compatibility.json: {message}; guide deploy"));
    27	    let config: Configuration = serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| bad(e.to_string()))?;
    28	    if config.since_release == 0 { return Err(bad("since_release must be a positive release label".into())); }
    29	    let path = project.root.join(&config.baseline);
    30	    let absolute = std::path::absolute(&path)?;
    31	    let origin = format!("resolved from {} at project root {}", project.root.join("snapback/client-compatibility.json").display(), project.root.display());
    32	    let baseline_error = |message| bad(format!("baseline {}: {message} ({origin})", absolute.display()));
    33	    if !path.exists() {
    34	        return Err(bad(format!("baseline {} not saved yet: does not exist ({origin})", absolute.display())));
    35	    }
    36	    let sources = sources(&path).map_err(|e| baseline_error(e.to_string()))?;
    37	    let baseline = snapback4_lang::compile(&sources);
    38	    if !baseline.diagnostics.is_empty() { return Err(baseline_error(format!("saved baseline does not compile: {:?}", baseline.diagnostics))); }
    39	    let warnings=snapback4_lang::client_contracts::compare(&baseline,bound,&format!("baseline {}",absolute.display()));
    40	    Ok((snapback4_lang::emit::typescript_spanning(bound, &baseline, config.since_release),warnings))
    41	}
    42	
    43	/// Shared schema-first ordering retains saved modules' original namespaces.
    44	pub(crate) fn sources(path: &std::path::Path) -> io::Result<Vec<(String, String)>> {
    45	    if !path.try_exists()? {return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::NotFound,format!("{}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist",path.display())));}
    46	    let bad = |message: &str| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, message);
    47	    let mut paths = if path.is_dir() {
    48	        std::fs::read_dir(path)?.map(|entry| entry.map(|entry| entry.path())).collect::<io::Result<Vec<_>>>()?
    49	            .into_iter().filter(|path| path.is_file() && path.extension().is_some_and(|extension| extension == "q")).collect::<Vec<_>>()
    50	    } else { vec![path.to_owned()] };
    51	    paths.sort_by_key(|path| (path.file_name().is_none_or(|name| name != "schema.q"), path.clone()));
    52	    if paths.is_empty() { return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::NotFound, "not saved yet: saved source directory has no Quarry .q sources")); }
    53	    let sources: Vec<(String, String)> = paths.into_iter().map(|path| {
    54	        let name = path.file_name().and_then(|name| name.to_str()).filter(|name| name.ends_with(".q"))
    55	            .ok_or_else(|| bad("saved sources need their original UTF-8 .q file names"))?;
    56	        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|e| io::Error::new(e.kind(), format!("saved source {}: {}{e}", path.display(), if e.kind()==io::ErrorKind::NotFound {"not saved yet: "}else{""})))?;
    57	        Ok((name.to_owned(), text))
    58	    }).collect::<io::Result<_>>()?;
    59	    if sources.iter().all(|(_, text)| text.trim().is_empty()) {
    60	        return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::NotFound, "not saved yet: saved Quarry sources are empty"));
    61	    }
    62	    Ok(sources)
    63	}

  1201	## 20. Visible rollback views (ROLL)
  1202	
  1203	Implementer: Codex for Charlie Cheever, 2026-10-22; commissioned for 0.4.39.
  1204	This amendment replaces opt-in bridging for declared domain widenings and
  1205	relaxed unique keys. Existing retention, metering, authority, immutable source,
  1206	and atomic activation laws continue to apply. SEC7 owns ordinary check gates
  1207	and ranking; ROLL owns deploy presentation and release selection.
  1208	
  1209	### 20.1 Prepared source and selection
  1210	
  1211	Deploy compares every selectable ordinary predecessor, even without sampled
  1212	widened rows. A widening without an applicable authored down requires a compiled
  1213	rollback view. All needed views must prepare successfully or deploy refuses.
  1214	View admission compares structural source domains (including text minima and
  1215	formats), nominal aliases, unique keys and caps; it never parses human advice
  1216	strings to decide whether a view is needed. A fitting authored scalar down
  1217	removes its covered value-domain risk; extraction/renames retain their typed
  1218	identity laws.
  1219	The compatible join is LLP 3000.000.000.002 §8: optional union, integer and text
  1220	bound union with identical format, enum union, recursively joined bounded lists,
  1221	and asset size-bound union with identical nominal kind, MIME and once policy.
  1222	Nullability and list cardinality join separately. This differs from extraction,
  1223	which requires full type equality, including size, optionality and cardinality.
  1224	References join only across explicit nominal identity renames. A mixed change
  1225	cannot enlarge any current shared-column domain with predecessor-only values:
  1226	current read/write predicates may rely on their schema-level impossibility.
  1227	The ordinary union view refuses even if that column is not the widening trigger.
  1228	A closed alternative may retain the complete current authored source closure
  1229	(schema, defaults, programs, helpers, jobs and authority), rather than synthesize
  1230	a union. It requires every predecessor table and column under the same spelling,
  1231	every predecessor operation name/kind, and current argument domains accepting
  1232	every predecessor input under the same closed compatibility rule below. It
  1233	retains the exact current domains and values, with no resurrection, translation
  1234	or old-only writes. Missing fields/names or incompatible inputs refuse. Current
  1235	result semantics are disclosed for old-UI review; current extra operations
  1236	remain current-authorized. Native/worker bundle qualification remains mandatory.
  1237	Otherwise author a compatible source and truthful migration before deploying. Enum order
  1238	and nominal rename spelling do not change this domain comparison. Domain
  1239	includes nullability, recursive list-element/cardinality bounds, text formats,
  1240	integer bounds, asset kind/size/MIME/once constraints; unsupported or incompatible
  1241	domain changes refuse rather than truncate or coerce. No conversion,
  1242	zero-money fallback, implicit business default or arbitrary row choice is inferred.
  1243	
  1244	The view retains predecessor operation names and argument contracts, including
  1245	existing authored operation aliases under their compilation law (same-module,
  1246	no alias chains). Every exported predecessor alias counts as an old name; a
  1247	current alias resolves to its actual authored target for input, guard, helper
  1248	and authority comparison. Renaming an export never drops its current guards. A current
  1249	require can be copied only with identical argument and entire transitive local
  1250	binding/helper meanings, lookup predicates and control-flow paths. A changed
  1251	unmodeled closure or guard depending on writes refuses. Canonical expression
  1252	comparison ignores source locations, never semantics. When a relaxed unique key invalidates an old lookup, or current guards require
  1253	an added field on a shared table, retain that field and its authored current
  1254	backfill. An affected operation whose current guard/workflow cannot be transported
  1255	unchanged may likewise reuse its current authored endpoint under the same closed
  1256	input-compatibility rule. Every current added field is retained on a shared table, even without a guard
  1257	mention; required fields retain their authored default/backfill and every old
  1258	write path must supply them through a compatible current authored endpoint or
  1259	refuse compilation. No missing-required-field write is permitted. All helpers in a reused workflow, including write,
  1260	return and system-scope dependencies, need identical transitive meanings. An already-authored current endpoint of the same name/kind whose
  1261	arguments accept every predecessor input may replace the affected old endpoint;
  1262	the initial comparison permits identical argument types, optional widening and
  1263	integer-bound widening and enum-superset widening, never narrowing or new required arguments. Retained
  1264	current backfill identity keeps its output custody active. This supplies authored
  1265	writes for the added required fields instead of inventing defaults; the generator never invents first-row
  1266	selection. The replacement's point/limited selection must retain the language's
  1267	deterministic total ordering, including its stable id tie-break; a source outside
  1268	that deterministic language fragment refuses. Missing or incompatible authored
  1269	endpoints refuse compilation.
  1270	
  1271	A generated visible view retains the current forward-migration declaration set
  1272	for shared output columns. It does not resurrect a retired predecessor transform
  1273	whose outputs already remain current columns in the compiled union. Historical
  1274	source and receipts remain intact. This preserves current native values and
  1275	migration custody instead of reinterpreting them with a retired input domain.
  1276	Transforms needed for fields removed from current source remain subject to the
  1277	ordinary truthful-projection and complete-adoption checks.
  1278	
  1279	A generated visible view omits a predecessor-only historical down declaration
  1280	when all its outputs remain current columns whose generated domains admit the
  1281	current native values. Its immutable original declaration and receipt evidence
  1282	remain historical; the view does not reenter that retired projection or restore
  1283	stale preimages over native values. Downs needed for removed fields remain.
  1284	Exact original-source selection retains its projection/conflict behavior. Writer
  1285	adoption still enforces full domains, lifetimes and zero-unacknowledged hiding.
  1286	
  1287	Shared tables retain current caps, immutability, final-existence constraints
  1288	and lifecycle guards. Generated source carries authored current constraints,
  1289	unique keys, expiry and opening declarations; the compiled view must preserve
  1290	current hard constraints, reference-deletion/lifetime shape and sync audience
  1291	or preparation refuses. Text constraints follow the explicit domain join, not
  1292	a separate implicit relaxation. Schema quotas cannot become weaker merely
  1293	because their current rule IR is unchanged. Unsupported structural transport
  1294	requires an authored compatible workflow; no arbitrary population choice or
  1295	new business default is generated.
  1296	
  1297	Shared tables retain the **current authored authority**, including transition
